# 🏛️ SCEAU — Bilan de la Version 1 (V1)

## 🚀 Statut : Déployé et Fonctionnel
L'application SCEAU (V1) est officiellement en ligne. Le code est stable, les interfaces communiquent, et la plomberie de stockage est branchée.

## 🛠️ Stack Technique
- **Frontend & Backend** : Next.js 16 (App Router), React, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS.
- **Base de données** : PostgreSQL hébergé sur Neon.
- **Stockage de fichiers** : Compatible protocole S3 (Actuellement sur Supabase, prêt à migrer vers Cloudflare R2 ou AWS en 5 minutes via variables d'environnement).
- **Ancrage Cryptographique** : OpenTimestamps (Ancrage des hashs dans la blockchain Bitcoin).
- **Sécurité** : Hachage côté client avec `@noble/hashes` (le fichier original ne quitte jamais l'appareil sans consentement).

## 🧩 Fonctionnalités Implémentées
1. **L'Atelier** : Scellement de fichiers, textes, audios et photos.
2. **Sceau Flash** : Preuve locale sans hébergement (pour les secrets industriels ou idées fugaces).
3. **Sceau Mystère** : Fichier masqué sur le feed jusqu'à obtention d'un certain nombre de "J'aime".
4. **Sceau Éphémère** : Disparaît du feed public après 24h (mais la preuve reste).
5. **Le Feed Global** : Réactions multiples (Like, Wow, Inspirant), Tendances, et boutons de partage.
6. **Le Cercle** : Système d'amis complet et système de "Défis" de création.
7. **Messagerie** : Messages privés, groupes de discussion et Pactes multi-signatures.
8. **Le Coffre** : 4 compartiments (Public, Amis, Privé, Flash) avec possibilité de déplacer les œuvres.
9. **Le Portfolio** : Génération de PDF modulaire (filtrage par date, par compartiment, avec intégration des liens).
10. **La preuve** : Manifeste des 3 piliers (Droit d'auteur, Antériorité, Journal des accès).

## ⚖️ La Philosophie (Rappel)
SCEAU ne réinvente pas la loi. SCEAU lui donne des yeux.
- **La technique est prête** : Chaque hash est gravé via OpenTimestamps.
- **La loi fera sa part** : La reconnaissance juridique viendra avec l'adoption et les partenariats (eIDAS, avocats).
- **L'adoption fera le reste** : Comme le cadenas HTTPS, SCEAU deviendra un standard invisible.

## 🔜 Prochaines Étapes (V2)
- Monétisation (Stripe) : Activation des murs payants (quotas de stockage, portfolio sans filigrane).
- Extension navigateur (Le badge URNE).
- API publique pour les plateformes tierces.

*Généré avec fierté le 06/11/2026.*

FICHIER : ./fix-db-final.sql

-- =====================================================
-- TABLE PROJECTS (Arbre de Merkle)
-- =====================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  merkle_root TEXT,
  ots_proof TEXT,
  parent_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Ajout des colonnes manquantes (si la table existait déjà)
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS title TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS description TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS merkle_root TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parent_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

-- Migration : si l'ancienne colonne "name" existe, on la copie vers "title"
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'projects' AND column_name = 'name') THEN
    UPDATE projects SET title = name WHERE title IS NULL AND name IS NOT NULL;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================
-- TABLE PROJECT_FILES (fichiers de chaque projet)
-- =====================================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS project_files (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  file_path TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- =====================================================
-- TABLE WEB_ARCHIVES (nouvelles colonnes)
-- =====================================================
ALTER TABLE web_archives ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS site_root TEXT;
ALTER TABLE web_archives ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pages_scanned INTEGER DEFAULT 1;
ALTER TABLE web_archives ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS scan_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE web_archives ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ai_summary TEXT;

-- =====================================================
-- INDEX (performance)
-- =====================================================
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_project_files_project ON project_files(project_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_projects_author ON projects(author_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS oracle_queries (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  url TEXT NOT NULL,
  question TEXT NOT NULL,
  answer TEXT,
  sources JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  captures_used INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  model TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_oracle_queries_user ON oracle_queries(user_id, created_at DESC);

=========================================
FICHIER : ./public/downloads/chrome-extension/popup.js
=========================================

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const pubkeyInput = document.getElementById('pubkey');
  const sealBtn = document.getElementById('sealBtn');
  const status = document.getElementById('status');

  chrome.storage.local.get(['sceau_pubkey'], (result) => {
    if (result.sceau_pubkey) pubkeyInput.value = result.sceau_pubkey;
  });

  sealBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const pubkey = pubkeyInput.value.trim();
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Veuillez entrer votre clé publique.");

    chrome.storage.local.set({ sceau_pubkey: pubkey });
    sealBtn.innerText = 'Envoi en cours...';

    chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true }, async (tabs) => {
      const url = tabs[0].url;
      
      try {
        const res = await fetch('https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/archive', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, url })
        });
        
        if (res.ok) {
          sealBtn.style.display = 'none';
          status.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
          const d = await res.json();
          alert("Erreur : " + d.error);
          sealBtn.innerText = 'Sceller cette page';
        }
      } catch (e) {
        alert("Erreur réseau. Vérifiez votre connexion.");
        sealBtn.innerText = 'Sceller cette page';
      }
    });
  });
});

FICHIER : ./public/sw.js
=========================================

// Service Worker de base pour la PWA SCEAU
self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  self.skipWaiting();
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(clients.claim());
});

// Écouteur pour les notifications Push (simulé pour le lancement)
self.addEventListener('push', function(event) {
  const data = event.data ? event.data.json() : { title: 'SCEAU', body: 'Nouvelle preuve gravée.' };
  
  const options = {
    body: data.body,
    icon: 'https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=S&background=E76F51&color=fff&size=192',
    badge: 'https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=S&background=E76F51&color=fff&size=96',
    vibrate: [100, 50, 100],
    data: { url: data.url || '/feed' }
  };

  event.waitUntil(
    self.registration.showNotification(data.title, options)
  );
});

// Clic sur la notification
self.addEventListener('notificationclick', function(event) {
  event.notification.close();
  event.waitUntil(
    clients.openWindow(event.notification.data.url)
  );
});

FICHIER : ./sceau-master-schema.sql

-- ============================================================
-- SCEAU — Schéma Master (idempotent, safe to run multiple times)
-- ============================================================

-- 1. USERS (déjà existante, on ajoute les colonnes manquantes)
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS email TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS password_hash TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS recovery_email TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reset_token TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reset_expiry TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS bio TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS role TEXT DEFAULT 'createur';
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS github_username TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS open_to_collaboration BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_pro BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS first_name TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS last_name TEXT;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS birth_date DATE;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS identity_changes_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS identity_history JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb;

-- 2. SEALS (déjà existante)
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS description TEXT;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art';
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS file_path TEXT;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS visibility TEXT DEFAULT 'public';
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_flash BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_mystery BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS unlock_likes INTEGER DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_ephemeral BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reveal_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_upgraded_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS project_id UUID;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS version_number INTEGER DEFAULT 1;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS challenge_id UUID;
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS work_type TEXT DEFAULT 'fichier';
ALTER TABLE seals ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS revision_status TEXT DEFAULT 'valide';

-- 3. PROJECTS (Arbre de Merkle) — création complète
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projects (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title TEXT,
  description TEXT,
  merkle_root TEXT,
  ots_proof TEXT,
  parent_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Migration : si l'ancienne colonne "name" existe, on la copie vers "title"
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'projects' AND column_name = 'name') THEN
    UPDATE projects SET title = name WHERE title IS NULL AND name IS NOT NULL;
  END IF;
END $$;

ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS title TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS description TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS merkle_root TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS parent_id UUID REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
ALTER TABLE projects ALTER COLUMN title SET NOT NULL;

-- 4. PROJECT_FILES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS project_files (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  file_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  file_path TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 5. PACTS (contrats multi-signatures)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pacts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  creator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  group_id UUID,
  required_signatures INTEGER DEFAULT 2,
  hash BYTEA,
  ots_proof TEXT,
  media_urls JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art',
  theme TEXT DEFAULT 'serieux',
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'en_attente',
  scelle_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS media_urls JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS theme TEXT DEFAULT 'serieux';
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS hash BYTEA;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS ots_proof TEXT;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS scelle_at TIMESTAMPTZ;
ALTER TABLE pacts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pact_hash BYTEA;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pact_signatures (
  pact_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pacts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (pact_id, user_id)
);
ALTER TABLE pact_signatures ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW();

-- 6. MESSAGES (DM)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  sender_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  receiver_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_audio BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 7. GROUPS
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS groups (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  creator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS group_members (
  group_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (group_id, user_id)
);

  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  group_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  sender_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_audio BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- 8. FRIENDSHIPS
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS friendships (
  requester_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

);

# 🏛️ SCEAU Protocol (v1.0)

Le protocole de preuve d'antériorité de SCEAU.
Permet d'ancrer l'empreinte cryptographique d'un fichier sur la blockchain Bitcoin.

## 🚀 Utilisation via l'API publique (Terminal)

Vous pouvez utiliser l'API REST gratuite pour sceller un document instantanément avec `curl` :

```bash
curl -X POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal \
  -F "file=@mon-document.pdf"

=========================================
FICHIER : ./sceau-protocol/spec/01-architecture.md
=========================================

# SCEAU Protocol — Architecture Générale

## 1. Le Principe
SCEAU est un protocole de preuve d'antériorité. Il sépare strictement **la preuve** (le hash et son ancrage) de **l'objet** (le fichier).

## 2. Les 3 Composants
1. **Le Hash (SHA-256)** : Une empreinte unique du fichier. Si un seul bit change, le hash change.
2. **L'Ancrage (OpenTimestamps)** : Le hash est envoyé à des serveurs "Calendar" qui l'agrègent et l'écrivent dans la blockchain Bitcoin.
3. **La Preuve (.ots)** : Un fichier cryptographique téléchargeable qui prouve mathématiquement que le hash existait au moment du bloc Bitcoin.


=========================================
FICHIER : ./sceau-protocol/spec/02-stockage.md
=========================================

# SCEAU Protocol — Le Modèle de Stockage

Le protocole ne stocke que des hashes. Où est le fichier ? Il y a 3 façons de faire selon le besoin.

## Modèle A : Stockage par l'Application SCEAU (Centralisé)
L'utilisateur dépose le fichier sur SCEAU. L'application calcule le hash, l'ancre sur Bitcoin, et stocke le fichier sur un serveur cloud (S3/R2).
* Lien de vérification : L'application associe le hash (Bitcoin) au `file_path` (le serveur cloud) dans sa base de données.

## Modèle B : Stockage par l'API (Développeurs)
Un site externe utilise l'API SCEAU. Il envoie son fichier, reçoit le hash et la preuve `.ots`.
* Lien de vérification : C'est le site externe qui héberge le fichier sur son propre serveur.

## Modèle C : Le Web3 et IPFS (Décentralisé)
Le fichier est stocké sur le réseau IPFS. Sur IPFS, le nom du fichier EST son hash cryptographique.
* Lien de vérification : Le hash SCEAU (Bitcoin) et le hash IPFS du fichier sont associés.

# SCEAU Protocol — API Publique v1

L'API permet à n'importe quel site web de sceller un document sans authentification.

## Endpoint
`POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal`

## Paramètres (Form-Data)
- `file` (obligatoire) : Le fichier binaire à sceller.

## Réponse (JSON)
```json
{
  "success": true,
  "protocol": "SCEAU v1.0",
  "document": {
    "name": "mon-contrat.pdf",
    "hash_sha256": "4b227777d4dd1..."
  },
  "bitcoin_anchor": {
    "secured": true,
    "ots_proof_base64": "AE9wZW5UaW1lc3RhbXBz..."
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./sceau-protocol/src/index.ts
=========================================

import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

/**
 * Calcule l'empreinte SHA-256 d'un fichier (Buffer ou Uint8Array).
 */
export function hashDocument(content: Uint8Array): string {
  const hash = sha256(content);
  return Buffer.from(hash).toString('hex');
}

/**
 * Ancre un hash sur la blockchain Bitcoin via OpenTimestamps.
 * Retourne la preuve .ots en base64, prête à être stockée.
 */
export async function anchorToBitcoin(hashHex: string): Promise<{ otsProof: string | null, calendars: string[] }> {
  const hashBytes = Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');
  const successes: string[] = [];
  let proof: Buffer | undefined;

  for (const url of CALENDARS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
        body: hashBytes,
      });
      if (!res.ok) continue;

      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      const magic = Buffer.from([0x00,0x4f,0x70,0x65,0x6e,0x54,0x69,0x6d,0x65,0x73,0x74,0x61,0x6d,0x70,0x73,0x00,0x00,0x50,0x72,0x6f,0x6f,0x66,0x00]);
      
      if (!buf.subarray(0, magic.length).equals(magic)) continue;

      successes.push(url);
      if (!proof) proof = buf;
    } catch (e) {
      // Calendar indisponible, on tente le suivant
    }
  }

  return {
    otsProof: proof ? proof.toString('base64') : null,
    calendars: successes
  };
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, pubkey } = await req.json();
    const result = await pool.query("INSERT INTO users (handle, pubkey) VALUES ($1, decode($2, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", [handle, pubkey]);
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}`,

  'src/app/api/seal/route.ts': `import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, title, pubkey } = await req.json();
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const protocolRes = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/v1/seal', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash, title }) });
    const protocolData = await protocolRes.json();
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title) VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4)", [hash, protocolData.timestamp, user.rows[0].id, title]);
    return NextResponse.json(protocolData);
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}`,

  'src/app/signup/page.tsx': `'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
export default function Signup() {
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle) { setStatus("Pseudo requis"); return; }
    setStatus("Création...");
    try {
      const secret = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const pubkey = ed25519.getPublicKey(secret);
      let pubkeyStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < pubkey.length; i++) pubkeyStr += String.fromCharCode(pubkey[i]);
      let secretStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < secret.length; i++) secretStr += String.fromCharCode(secret[i]);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_secret', btoa(secretStr));
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', btoa(pubkeyStr));
      const res = await fetch('/api/signup', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ handle, pubkey: btoa(pubkeyStr) }) });
      if (res.ok) setStatus("Compte créé ! Va sur /seal"); else setStatus("Erreur");
    } catch (e) { setStatus("Erreur réseau"); }
  }
  return (<main className="p-10 max-w-md mx-auto"><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Créer un compte</h1><input type="text" placeholder="Pseudo" className="border p-2 w-full mb-4 text-black" value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} /><button onClick={handleSignup} className="bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded w-full">Créer</button><p className="mt-4 font-bold text-center text-black">{status}</p></main>);
}`,

  'src/app/seal/page.tsx': `'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
export default function Seal() {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  async function handleSeal() {
    if (!file || !title) { setStatus("Fichier et titre requis"); return; }
    setStatus("Calcul...");
    try {
      const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
      const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
      let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
      const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
      if (!pubkey) { setStatus("Connecte-toi d'abord"); return; }
      setStatus("Envoi...");
      const res = await fetch('/api/seal', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: btoa(hashStr), title, pubkey }) });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) setStatus("Succès ! ID : " + data.seal_id.slice(0, 8)); else setStatus("Erreur");
    } catch (e) { setStatus("Erreur système"); }
  }
  return (<main className="p-10 max-w-md mx-auto"><h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Sceller un fichier</h1><input type="text" placeholder="Titre" className="border p-2 w-full mb-4 text-black" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} /><input type="file" className="mb-4 block w-full text-sm" onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} /><button onClick={handleSeal} className="bg-green-600 text-white p-2 rounded w-full">Sceller</button><p className="mt-4 font-bold text-center text-black">{status}</p></main>);
}`,

  'stub-protocol.js': `const http = require('http');
const crypto = require('crypto');
http.createServer((req, res) => {
  if (req.method === 'POST' && req.url === '/v1/seal') {
    res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });
    res.end(JSON.stringify({ seal_id: crypto.randomUUID(), timestamp: new Date().toISOString(), anchor_tx: 'tx-' + crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex') }));
  } else { res.writeHead(404); res.end(); }
}).listen(8080, () => console.log('✅ Stub prêt sur 8080'));`,

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

const MAX_PAGES = 10;
const FREE_LIMIT = 5;  // Sites gratuits (pas pages)
const PRO_LIMIT = 50;

function isSafeUrl(urlString: string): boolean {
  try {
    const url = new URL(urlString);
    if (url.protocol !== 'http:' && url.protocol !== 'https:') return false;
    const h = url.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (h === 'localhost' || h === '127.0.0.1' || h === '::1') return false;
    if (/^10\./.test(h)) return false;
    if (/^192\.168\./.test(h)) return false;
    if (/^172\.(1[6-9]|2[0-9]|3[0-1])\./.test(h)) return false;
    if (/^169\.254\./.test(h)) return false;
    return true;
  } catch { return false; }
}

function cleanHtmlToText(html: string): string {
  return html
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<style\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/style>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '\n')
    .replace(/\n\s*\n/g, '\n')
    .trim();
}

function extractPageTitle(html: string, fallbackUrl: string): string {
  // Cherche <title>...</title>
  const titleMatch = html.match(/<title[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/title>/i);
  if (titleMatch && titleMatch[1]) {
    const clean = titleMatch[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (clean.length > 0 && clean.length < 200) return clean;
  }
  // Cherche <h1>...</h1>
  const h1Match = html.match(/<h1[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/h1>/i);
  if (h1Match && h1Match[1]) {
    const clean = h1Match[1].replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (clean.length > 0 && clean.length < 100) return clean;
  }
  // Fallback : hostname + pathname
  try {
    const u = new URL(fallbackUrl);
    if (u.pathname === '/' || u.pathname === '') return u.hostname;
    return `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return fallbackUrl;
  }
}

function extractInternalLinks(html: string, baseUrl: string): string[] {
  const links: Set<string> = new Set();
  const regex = /<a[^>]+href=["']([^"']+)["']/gi;
  let match;
  const base = new URL(baseUrl);
  while ((match = regex.exec(html)) !== null) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(match[1], baseUrl);
      if (url.hostname === base.hostname && url.protocol.startsWith('http')) {
        url.hash = '';
        url.search = ''; // ignore query strings pour éviter les doublons
        links.add(url.toString());
      }
    } catch { /* ignore */ }
  }
  return Array.from(links);
}

async function fetchSitemap(siteRoot: string): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${siteRoot}/sitemap.xml`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-Bot/1.0' },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
    });
    if (!res.ok) return [];
    const xml = await res.text();
    const matches = xml.match(/<loc>([^<]+)<\/loc>/g) || [];
    return matches.map(m => m.replace(/<\/?loc>/g, '').trim()).slice(0, MAX_PAGES);
  } catch { return []; }
}

async function scanPage(url: string): Promise<{ text: string; hash: string; title: string } | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-Bot/1.0' },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
      redirect: 'follow',
    });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const html = await res.text();
    if (html.length > 5 * 1024 * 1024) return null;
    const text = cleanHtmlToText(html);
    const hash = Buffer.from(sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(text))).toString('hex');
    const title = extractPageTitle(html, url);
    return { text, hash, title };
  } catch { return null; }
}

function generateDiff(oldText: string, newText: string) {
  const oldLines = oldText.split('\n');
  const newLines = newText.split('\n');
  const diff = [];
  const maxLines = Math.max(oldLines.length, newLines.length);
  let changesCount = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < maxLines; i++) {
    const o = oldLines[i] || '';
    const n = newLines[i] || '';
    if (o !== n) {
      diff.push({ type: 'removed', text: o.trim() });
      diff.push({ type: 'added', text: n.trim() });
      changesCount++;
    } else if (o.trim() !== '') {
      diff.push({ type: 'unchanged', text: o.trim() });
    }
  }
  return { changesCount, details: diff.slice(0, 100) };
}

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé manquante' }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, is_pro FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;
    const isPro = user.rows[0].is_pro || false;

    // Récupère toutes les pages, puis regroupe par site_root côté serveur
    let pages;
    try {
      pages = await pool.query(`
        SELECT id, url, site_root, title, last_status, last_checked_at, created_at, diff_data, pages_scanned, scan_count, ai_summary
        FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC
      `, [userId]);
    } catch (colErr) {
      // Fallback si colonnes manquantes
      pages = await pool.query(`
        SELECT id, url, title, last_status, last_checked_at, created_at, diff_data
        FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC
      `, [userId]);
    }

    // Regroupement par site_root (site principal)
    const sitesMap = new Map<string, any>();
    for (const page of pages.rows) {
      const siteRoot = page.site_root || (() => {
        try { return new URL(page.url).origin; } catch { return page.url; }
      })();
      
      if (!sitesMap.has(siteRoot)) {
        sitesMap.set(siteRoot, {
          site_root: siteRoot,
          main_id: page.id,          // la page principale (première = l'URL ajoutée)
          main_title: page.site_root === null ? page.title : null, // à remplir
          main_url: page.url,
          pages_count: 0,
          last_checked_at: page.last_checked_at,
          created_at: page.created_at,
          scan_count: page.scan_count || 1,
          has_modifications: false,
          pages: [],
        });
      }
      
      const site = sitesMap.get(siteRoot);
      site.pages_count++;
      site.pages.push(page);
      
      // Le main_id doit être celui de la page racine (pathname === '/')
      try {
        const u = new URL(page.url);
        if (u.pathname === '/' || u.pathname === '') {
          site.main_id = page.id;
          site.main_title = page.title;
          site.main_url = page.url;
        }
      } catch { /* ignore */ }
      
      if (page.last_status === 'modified') site.has_modifications = true;
      if (new Date(page.last_checked_at) > new Date(site.last_checked_at)) {
        site.last_checked_at = page.last_checked_at;
      }
    }

    // Si le titre principal n'a pas été trouvé, fallback sur le hostname
    for (const site of sitesMap.values()) {
      if (!site.main_title) {
        try { site.main_title = new URL(site.site_root).hostname; }
        catch { site.main_title = site.site_root; }
      }
    }

    const sitesArray = Array.from(sitesMap.values());

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      archives: sitesArray, 
      is_pro: isPro,
      limit: isPro ? PRO_LIMIT : FREE_LIMIT,
      total_sites: sitesArray.length,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[archive GET]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pubkey, url, action } = await req.json();
  if (!pubkey || !url) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, is_pro FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;
    const isPro = user.rows[0].is_pro;

    // === VÉRIFICATION (action 'check') ===
    if (action === 'check') {
      const archive = await pool.query(
        "SELECT id, url, last_hash, last_content FROM web_archives WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2",
        [url, userId]
      );
      if (archive.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Archive introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
      
      const targetUrl = archive.rows[0].url;
      const oldHash = archive.rows[0].last_hash;
      const oldContent = archive.rows[0].last_content || '';

      const scan = await scanPage(targetUrl);
      if (!scan) {
        await pool.query(
          "UPDATE web_archives SET last_status = 'error', last_checked_at = NOW(), scan_count = COALESCE(scan_count, 0) + 1 WHERE id = $1",
          [url]
        );
        return NextResponse.json({ status: 'error' });
      }

      if (oldHash !== scan.hash) {
        const diffResult = generateDiff(oldContent, scan.text);
        await pool.query(
          `UPDATE web_archives 
           SET last_status = 'modified', last_checked_at = NOW(), diff_data = $1, 
               last_hash = $2, last_content = $3, scan_count = COALESCE(scan_count, 0) + 1 
           WHERE id = $4`,
          [JSON.stringify(diffResult), scan.hash, scan.text, url]
        );
        return NextResponse.json({ status: 'modified', changes: diffResult.changesCount });
      } else {
        await pool.query(
          "UPDATE web_archives SET last_status = 'intact', last_checked_at = NOW(), scan_count = COALESCE(scan_count, 0) + 1 WHERE id = $1",
          [url]
        );
        return NextResponse.json({ status: 'intact' });
      }
    }

    // === AJOUT D'UN NOUVEAU SITE ===
    if (!isSafeUrl(url)) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'URL invalide ou non autorisée.' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const urlObj = new URL(url);
    const siteRoot = `${urlObj.protocol}//${urlObj.hostname}`;

    // COMPTAGE PAR SITE (pas par page) : combien de sites distincts ?
    const sitesCountQuery = await pool.query(
      "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT site_root) as count FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 AND site_root IS NOT NULL",
      [userId]
    );
    const sitesCount = parseInt(sitesCountQuery.rows[0].count || 0);
    const maxLimit = isPro ? PRO_LIMIT : FREE_LIMIT;

    // Vérifie si ce site existe déjà
    const existingSite = await pool.query(
      "SELECT id FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 AND site_root = $2 LIMIT 1",
      [userId, siteRoot]
    );

    if (existingSite.rows.length === 0 && sitesCount >= maxLimit) {
      return NextResponse.json({ 
        error: `Quota atteint (${maxLimit} sites max). Passez à SCEAU PRO pour en ajouter plus.` 
      }, { status: 403 });
    }

    // Si le site existe déjà, on ajoute juste les nouvelles pages
    // Sinon, on crawle comme d'habitude

    let pages: string[] = [];
    const sitemapPages = await fetchSitemap(siteRoot);
    if (sitemapPages.length > 0) {
      pages = sitemapPages;
    } else {
      const rootHtml = await fetch(url, {
        headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-Bot/1.0' },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000),
      }).then(r => r.ok ? r.text() : null).catch(() => null);

      if (!rootHtml) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Impossible de joindre le site.' }, { status: 400 });
      }
      const internalLinks = extractInternalLinks(rootHtml, url);
      pages = [url, ...internalLinks.filter(l => l !== url)].slice(0, MAX_PAGES);
    }

    const scanResults = await Promise.all(pages.map(p => scanPage(p)));
    
    let insertedCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < pages.length; i++) {
      const pageUrl = pages[i];
      const pageData = scanResults[i];
      if (!pageData) continue;
      
      // Vérifie si cette page existe déjà
      const existing = await pool.query(
        "SELECT id FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 AND url = $2",
        [userId, pageUrl]
      );
      
      if (existing.rows.length === 0) {
        await pool.query(`
          INSERT INTO web_archives (user_id, url, site_root, title, last_hash, last_content, last_status, pages_scanned, scan_count)
          VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, 'intact', $7, 1)
        `, [userId, pageUrl, siteRoot, pageData.title, pageData.hash, pageData.text, pages.length]);
        insertedCount++;
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      success: true, 
      pages_scanned: insertedCount,
      total_pages: pages.length,
      site_root: siteRoot,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('[archive POST]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: e?.message || 'Erreur serveur.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  const id = searchParams.get('id');

  if (!pubkey || !id) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Si c'est un site entier, on supprime toutes ses pages
    const site = await pool.query(
      "SELECT site_root FROM web_archives WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2",
      [id, user.rows[0].id]
    );
    
    if (site.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    if (site.rows[0].site_root) {
      await pool.query(
        "DELETE FROM web_archives WHERE user_id = $1 AND site_root = $2",
        [user.rows[0].id, site.rows[0].site_root]
      );
    } else {
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM web_archives WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2", [id, user.rows[0].id]);
    }
    
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';

// GET : Lister les clés de l'utilisateur (masquées)
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    const result = await pool.query(
      "SELECT id, name, created_at, last_used_at FROM api_keys WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC",
      [user.rows[0].id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ keys: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// POST : Générer une nouvelle clé
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pubkey, name } = await req.json();
  if (!pubkey || !name) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Générer une clé aléatoire (sceau_live_ + 32 caractères hexadécimaux)
    const rawKey = `sceau_live_${crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`;
    
    // On ne stocke que le hash SHA-256 de la clé en base de données
    const keyHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawKey).digest('hex');

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO api_keys (user_id, key_hash, name) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [user.rows[0].id, keyHash, name]
    );

    // On retourne la clé EN CLAIR une seule fois. Elle ne sera plus jamais visible.
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, key: rawKey });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// DELETE : Révoquer une clé
export async function DELETE(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  const id = searchParams.get('id');

  if (!pubkey || !id) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    await pool.query("DELETE FROM api_keys WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = $2", [id, user.rows[0].id]);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { email } = await req.json();
  if (!email) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const expiry = new Date(Date.now() + 3600000); 

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET reset_token = $1, reset_expiry = $2 WHERE email = $3", [token, expiry, email]);

  const resetLink = `${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'http://localhost:3000'}/reset-password?token=${token}`;
  
  try {
    const resendKey = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY;
    if (!resendKey) {
      // Si pas de clé, on simule l'envoi pour le développement
      console.log("⚠️ Pas de clé Resend. Lien de test :", resetLink);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Lien généré (voir console)" });
    }

    const resend = new Resend(resendKey);
    await resend.emails.send({
      from: 'SCEAU <onboarding@resend.dev>',
      to: email,
      subject: "Réinitialisation de votre mot de passe",
      html: `<p>Bonjour,</p><p>Voici votre lien : <a href="${resetLink}">Réinitialiser mon mot de passe</a></p>`,
    });
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, message: "Email envoyé !" });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur d'envoi d'email" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

const loginCache = new Map<string, { count: number, resetAt: number }>();

function checkLimit(ip: string) {
  const now = Date.now();
  let e = loginCache.get(ip);
  if (!e || e.resetAt < now) { e = { count: 0, resetAt: now + 60000 }; loginCache.set(ip, e); }
  if (e.count >= 5) return false;
  e.count++;
  return true;
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for') || 'unknown';
  if (!checkLimit(ip)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Trop de tentatives. Réessayez dans 1 minute.' }, { status: 429 });

  try {
    const { email, password } = await req.json();

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1", [email]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.rows[0].password_hash);
    if (!isValid) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Email ou mot de passe incorrect' }, { status: 401 });
    }

    // On renvoie le handle et la clé publique (convertie en base64)
    return NextResponse.json({
      handle: user.rows[0].handle,
      pubkey: Buffer.from(user.rows[0].pubkey).toString('base64')
    });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/api/auth/reset-password/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { token, newPassword } = await req.json();
  if (!token || !newPassword) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs requis' }, { status: 400 });

  const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE reset_token = $1 AND reset_expiry > NOW()", [token]);
  if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Lien invalide ou expiré' }, { status: 400 });

  const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
  const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(newPassword, salt);

  await pool.query("UPDATE users SET password_hash = $1, reset_token = NULL, reset_expiry = NULL WHERE id = $2", [password_hash, user.rows[0].id]);

  return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { handle, email, password, pubkey } = await req.json();

    if (!handle || !email || !password || !pubkey) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tous les champs sont requis' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
    const password_hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

    const result = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO users (handle, email, password_hash, pubkey) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, decode($4, 'base64')) RETURNING id, handle", 
      [handle, email, password_hash, pubkey]
    );
    
    return NextResponse.json(result.rows[0]);
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur d'inscription:", error);
    if (error.code === '23505') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Ce pseudo ou cet email est déjà pris' }, { status: 409 });
    }
    // On renvoie le VRAI message d'erreur pour le débogage
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message || 'Erreur serveur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/api/challenges/[id]/route.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  try {
    const challenge = await pool.query("SELECT * FROM challenges WHERE id = $1", [id]);
    if (challenge.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const entries = await pool.query(`
      SELECT e.id as entry_id, s.id as seal_id, s.title, s.description, s.file_path, s.category, 
      CASE WHEN $2 = true THEN 'Anonyme' ELSE u.handle END as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_votes WHERE entry_id = e.id) as votes
      FROM challenge_entries e
      JOIN seals s ON e.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON e.user_id = u.id
      WHERE e.challenge_id = $1
      ORDER BY votes DESC
    `, [id, challenge.rows[0].is_anonymous]);

    return NextResponse.json({ challenge: challenge.rows[0], entries: entries.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Date limite dans 7 jours par défaut
    const deadline = new Date();
    deadline.setDate(deadline.getDate() + 7);

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenges (title, description, creator_id, deadline, is_anonymous) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)",
      [title, description, user.rows[0].id, deadline, is_anonymous]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM challenge_entries WHERE challenge_id = c.id) as entries_count

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { challenge_id, entry_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const voterId = user.rows[0].id;

    // Vérifier qu'il ne vote pas pour lui-même
    const entry = await pool.query("SELECT user_id FROM challenge_entries WHERE id = $1", [entry_id]);
    if (entry.rows.length > 0 && entry.rows[0].user_id === voterId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Tu ne peux pas voter pour ta propre œuvre !' }, { status: 403 });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO challenge_votes (challenge_id, voter_id, entry_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING",
      [challenge_id, voterId, entry_id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/comments/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

// Lire les commentaires d'une œuvre
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'ID manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT c.id, c.content, c.created_at, u.handle as author
      FROM comments c JOIN users u ON c.user_id = u.id
      WHERE c.seal_id = $1 ORDER BY c.created_at ASC
    `, [sealId]);
    return NextResponse.json({ comments: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

// Ajouter un commentaire
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, content } = await req.json();
  if (!seal_id || !pubkey || !content) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id, handle FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const myId = user.rows[0].id;

    await pool.query("INSERT INTO comments (seal_id, user_id, content) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)", [seal_id, myId, content]);

    // Créer une notification pour l'auteur de l'œuvre
    const seal = await pool.query("SELECT author_id, title FROM seals WHERE id = $1", [seal_id]);
    if (seal.rows.length > 0 && seal.rows[0].author_id !== myId) {
      await pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO notifications (user_id, actor_id, type, message, link) VALUES ($1, $2, 'comment', $3, $4)",
        [seal.rows[0].author_id, myId, `a commenté ton œuvre "${seal.rows[0].title}"`, `/o/${seal_id}`]
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

export async function GET() {

             u.handle as author,
             s.id as seal_id, s.title as seal_title
      FROM attachments a 
      JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      JOIN seals s ON a.seal_id = s.id
      WHERE a.parent_id IS NULL 
      ORDER BY RANDOM() 
      LIMIT 50
    `);

    console.error('[creations/global]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { creation_id, pubkey } = await req.json();
    if (!creation_id || !pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // Vérifier si le like existe déjà
    const existing = await pool.query("SELECT 1 FROM creation_likes WHERE attachment_id = $1 AND user_id = $2", [creation_id, userId]);
    
    if (existing.rows.length > 0) {
      // On retire le like
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM creation_likes WHERE attachment_id = $1 AND user_id = $2", [creation_id, userId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ liked: false });
    } else {
      // On ajoute le like
      await pool.query("INSERT INTO creation_likes (attachment_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [creation_id, userId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ liked: true });
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creation_like]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/creations/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { safeKeyPart } from '@/lib/safe-key';
import { requireS3 } from '@/lib/env';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

// GET : Récupérer les créations d'un sceau (ou d'une création si parent_id est fourni)
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  const parentId = searchParams.get('parent_id');

  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'seal_id manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    let sql = `
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, a.parent_id, u.handle as author, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM creation_likes WHERE attachment_id = a.id) as likes_count
      FROM attachments a 
      JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      WHERE a.seal_id = $1
    `;
    const params: any[] = [sealId];

    if (parentId) {
      sql += " AND a.parent_id = $2";
      params.push(parentId);
    } else {
      sql += " AND a.parent_id IS NULL"; // On ne récupère que le niveau principal
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT 50";
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ creations: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creations GET]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// POST : Créer une nouvelle création (vidéo, image, ou texte)
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const seal_id = data.get('seal_id') as string;
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const content = data.get('content') as string || '';
    const type = data.get('type') as string || 'text';
    const parent_id = data.get('parent_id') as string || null;
    const file = data.get('file') as File | null;

    if (!seal_id || !pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    let media_url: string | null = null;

    if (file && file.size > 0) {
      // === SÉCURITÉ : Vérification de l'extension ===
      const allowedExtensions = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp', 'mp4', 'webm', 'mp3', 'wav', 'ogg', 'pdf', 'txt'];
      const fileName = file.name.toLowerCase();
      const ext = fileName.split('.').pop() || '';
      
      if (!allowedExtensions.includes(ext)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ 
          error: `Format de fichier interdit (.${ext}).` 
        }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const { endpoint, bucket } = requireS3();
      const key = `creations/${safeKeyPart(seal_id.slice(0, 8))}/${Date.now()}-${safeKeyPart(file.name)}`;
      
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: bucket,
        Key: key,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
      }));
      media_url = `${endpoint}/${bucket}/${key}`;
    }

    const res = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO attachments (seal_id, user_id, content, type, media_url, parent_id)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6) RETURNING id`,
      [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content, type, media_url, parent_id]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, id: res.rows[0].id });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creations POST]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur lors de la création' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/cron/archive-scan/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { Resend } from 'resend';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 60;

function cleanHtmlToText(html: string): string {
  return html
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<style\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/style>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '\n')
    .replace(/\n\s*\n/g, '\n')
    .trim();
}

function hashContent(content: string): string {
  return Buffer.from(sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(content))).toString('hex');
}

function generateDiff(oldText: string, newText: string) {
  const oldLines = oldText.split('\n');
  const newLines = newText.split('\n');
  const diff = [];
  const maxLines = Math.max(oldLines.length, newLines.length);
  let changesCount = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < maxLines; i++) {
    const oldLine = oldLines[i] || '';
    const newLine = newLines[i] || '';
    if (oldLine !== newLine) {
      diff.push({ type: 'removed', text: oldLine.trim() });
      diff.push({ type: 'added', text: newLine.trim() });
      changesCount++;
    } else if (oldLine.trim() !== '') {
      diff.push({ type: 'unchanged', text: oldLine.trim() });
    }
  }
  return { changesCount, details: diff.slice(0, 100) };
}

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization');
  if (process.env.CRON_SECRET && authHeader !== `Bearer ${process.env.CRON_SECRET}`) {
    return new NextResponse('Unauthorized', { status: 401 });
  }

  try {
    const archives = await pool.query(`
      SELECT a.id, a.url, a.last_hash, a.last_content, u.email 
      FROM web_archives a 
      JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
    `);
    
    const resend = process.env.RESEND_API_KEY ? new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY) : null;
    let scanned = 0;
    let modifiedCount = 0;

    for (const archive of archives.rows) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(archive.url, { 
          headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-Archive-Bot/1.0' },
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10000)
        });
        
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Site inaccessible');
        
        const html = await res.text();
        if (html.length > 5 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('Contenu trop lourd');
        
        const newText = cleanHtmlToText(html);
        const newHash = hashContent(newText);

        if (archive.last_hash !== newHash) {
          const diffResult = generateDiff(archive.last_content || '', newText);
          
          await pool.query(
            "UPDATE web_archives SET last_status = 'modified', last_checked_at = NOW(), diff_data = $1, last_hash = $2, last_content = $3 WHERE id = $4",
            [JSON.stringify(diffResult), newHash, newText, archive.id]
          );

          if (resend && archive.email) {
            await resend.emails.send({
              from: 'SCEAU Archive <alerte@sceau-app.vercel.app>',
              to: archive.email,
              subject: `🚨 Modification détectée sur ${archive.url}`,
              html: `
                <div style="font-family: sans-serif; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto;">
                  <h2 style="color: #E76F51;">SCEAU Archive</h2>
                  <p>Une modification a été détectée sur la page que vous surveillez :</p>
                  <p><a href="${archive.url}" style="background: #eee; padding: 10px; display: block; border-radius: 8px; color: #333; text-decoration: none;">${archive.url}</a></p>
                  <p><strong>${diffResult.changesCount} changements</strong> ont été trouvés.</p>
                  <a href="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/archive" style="display: inline-block; background: #1A1513; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; border-radius: 8px; text-decoration: none; margin-top: 20px;">Voir le Diff complet</a>
                </div>
              `
            });
          }
          modifiedCount++;
        } else {
          await pool.query(
            "UPDATE web_archives SET last_status = 'intact', last_checked_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1",
            [archive.id]
          );
        }
        scanned++;
      } catch (e) {
        // En cas d'erreur, on marque en erreur
        await pool.query(
          "UPDATE web_archives SET last_status = 'error', last_checked_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1",
          [archive.id]
        );
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, scanned, modifiedCount });
  } catch (e) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Cron Error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/download/[id]/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { GetObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { Readable } from 'node:stream';
import { extractS3Key } from '@/lib/safe-key';
import { apiError } from '@/lib/api-error';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

function encodeContentDisposition(filename: string): string {
  const asciiFallback = filename
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '_')
    .replace(/["\\]/g, '_');
  return `attachment; filename="${asciiFallback}"; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
}

async function toWebStream(body: any): Promise<ReadableStream> {
  if (body instanceof Readable) return Readable.toWeb(body) as unknown as ReadableStream;
  if (body && typeof body.getReader === 'function') return body as ReadableStream;
  if (body && typeof body[Symbol.asyncIterator] === 'function') {
    return new ReadableStream({
      async start(controller) {
        try {
          for await (const chunk of body) controller.enqueue(chunk);
          controller.close();
        } catch (e) { controller.error(e); }
      },
    });
  }
  throw new Error('Body S3 non supporté');
}

export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const viewerPubkey = searchParams.get('viewer');

  try {
    const res = await pool.query(
      "SELECT file_path FROM seals WHERE id = $1 AND visibility = 'public'",
      [id]
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0 || !res.rows[0].file_path) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const key = extractS3Key(res.rows[0].file_path);
    if (!key) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé S3 invalide' }, { status: 500 });

    const obj = await r2Client.send(new GetObjectCommand({
      Bucket: process.env.S3_BUCKET,
      Key: key,
    }));
    if (!obj.Body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Fichier introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    if (viewerPubkey) {
      pool.query(
        "INSERT INTO access_log (seal_id, viewer_id, action, context) SELECT $1, id, 'download', 'public' FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2",
        [id, viewerPubkey]
      ).catch((e) => console.warn('[download] log échoué', e));
    }

    const webStream = await toWebStream(obj.Body);
    const filename = key.split('/').pop() || 'file';

    return new NextResponse(webStream, {
      status: 200,
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': obj.ContentType || 'application/octet-stream',
        'Content-Disposition': encodeContentDisposition(filename),
        'Content-Length': obj.ContentLength ? String(obj.ContentLength) : '',
        'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=0, must-revalidate',
      },
    });
  } catch (e) {
    return apiError(e, 'Erreur lors du téléchargement');
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] });
    
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM favorites f
      JOIN seals s ON f.seal_id = s.id
      JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE f.user_id = $1 ORDER BY f.created_at DESC
    `, [user.rows[0].id]);
    return NextResponse.json({ favorites: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ favorites: [] }); }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { seal_id, pubkey, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'add' | 'remove'
  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    if (action === 'remove') {
      await pool.query("DELETE FROM favorites WHERE user_id = $1 AND seal_id = $2", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    } else {
      await pool.query("INSERT INTO favorites (user_id, seal_id) VALUES ($1, $2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING", [user.rows[0].id, seal_id]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/feed/friends/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');

  if (!myHandle) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });

  try {
    // 1. On trouve l'ID de l'utilisateur
    const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
    if (me.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const myId = me.rows[0].id;

    // 2. On trouve les gens avec qui j'ai discuté (mes contacts)
    const contactsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT DISTINCT receiver_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE sender_id = $1
      UNION
      SELECT DISTINCT sender_id as contact_id FROM messages WHERE receiver_id = $1
    `, [myId]);

    if (contactsQuery.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: [] });
    const contactIds = contactsQuery.rows.map(r => r.contact_id);

    // 3. On récupère leurs dernières œuvres publiques (5 max)
    const seals = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.author_id = ANY($1) AND s.is_public = TRUE
      ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 5
    `, [contactIds]);

    return NextResponse.json({ friends_seals: seals.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.description, s.category, s.timestamp,
             encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
             s.file_path, s.is_hosted
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.visibility = 'public'
        AND s.is_mystery = FALSE
        AND s.is_ephemeral = FALSE
      ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1
    `);
    if (result.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ seal: null, reason: 'empty' });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: result.rows[0] });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[random]', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ seal: null, reason: 'error' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/feed/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const myHandle = searchParams.get('me');
  const category = searchParams.get('category');

  try {
    let params: any[] = [];
    let whereClause = "s.visibility = 'public'";

    if (category && category !== 'all') {
      params.push(category);
      whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length}`;
    }

    if (myHandle) {
      const me = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [myHandle]);
      if (me.rows.length > 0) {
        const myId = me.rows[0].id;
        const friendsRes = await pool.query(`
          SELECT requester_id as id FROM friendships WHERE addressee_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
          UNION
          SELECT addressee_id as id FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND status = 'accepted'
        `, [myId]);
        const friendIds = friendsRes.rows.map(r => r.id);
        if (friendIds.length > 0) {
          params.push(friendIds);
          whereClause = `(s.visibility = 'public' OR (s.visibility = 'friends' AND s.author_id = ANY($${params.length})))`;
          if (category && category !== 'all') {
             whereClause += ` AND s.category = $${params.length - 1}`; // On garde le filtre catégorie
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const sql = `
      SELECT s.id, 
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true THEN '🔒 Sceau Mystère' ELSE s.title END as title,
        CASE WHEN s.is_mystery = true AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) < s.unlock_likes THEN null ELSE s.file_path END as file_path,
        s.is_mystery, s.unlock_likes, s.is_flash, s.is_ephemeral, s.ots_proof,
        s.description, s.category, s.timestamp, s.visibility, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author,
        s.project_id, s.version_number,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'like') as likes_like,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'wow') as likes_wow,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id AND reaction_type = 'inspired') as likes_inspired
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE ${whereClause} ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 30
    `;
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ seals: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/feed/trends/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // On récupère les œuvres publiques de la semaine, triées par nombre de likes
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, s.location, u.handle as author,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM likes WHERE seal_id = s.id) as likes_count
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE AND s.timestamp >= NOW() - INTERVAL '7 days'
      ORDER BY likes_count DESC, s.timestamp DESC
      LIMIT 3
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ trends: result.rows });
  } catch (error) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/friends/respond/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { me, target, action } = await req.json(); // action: 'accept' | 'reject'
  try {
    const meId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [me])).rows[0].id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE handle = $1", [target])).rows[0].id;

    if (action === 'accept') {
      await pool.query(`
        UPDATE friendships SET status = 'accepted' 
        WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    } else {
      await pool.query(`
        DELETE FROM friendships WHERE requester_id = $1 AND addressee_id = $2
      `, [targetId, meId]);
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/friends/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
    let me = searchParams.get('me');
    const search = searchParams.get('search');
    
    if (!me) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non connecté' }, { status: 401 });
    // NOUVEAU : On nettoie le pseudo au cas où il y aurait un @
    me = me.replace('@', '');

    const myIdRes = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE LOWER(handle) = LOWER($1)", [me]);
    if (myIdRes.rows.length === 0) {
      // Message plus clair pour le débogage
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Ton compte n'existe pas dans la base de données. Déconnecte-toi et reconnecte-toi." }, { status: 404 });
    }
    const myId = myIdRes.rows[0].id;

    if (search) {
      const users = await pool.query(`
        SELECT handle, role FROM users 
        WHERE handle ILIKE $1 AND id != $2 LIMIT 10
      `, [`%${search.replace('@', '')}%`, myId]);
      return NextResponse.json({ searchResults: users.rows });
    }

    const friendsQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT 
        u.id, 
        u.handle,
        (SELECT title FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal,
        (SELECT id FROM seals WHERE author_id = u.id AND visibility = 'public' ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1) as last_seal_id
      FROM friendships f
      JOIN users u ON u.id = CASE WHEN f.requester_id = $1 THEN f.addressee_id ELSE f.requester_id END
      WHERE (f.requester_id = $1 OR f.addressee_id = $1) AND f.status = 'accepted'
    `, [myId]);

    const receivedQuery = await pool.query(`
      SELECT u.handle FROM friendships f 
      JOIN users u ON u.id = f.requester_id
      WHERE f.addressee_id = $1 AND f.status = 'pending'
    `, [myId]);

    return NextResponse.json({ 
      friends: friendsQuery.rows, 
      received: receivedQuery.rows 
    });

  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error("Erreur API Friends:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Erreur base de données : " + error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { me, target } = await req.json();
    
    // Nettoyage des pseudos
    const myId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE LOWER(handle) = LOWER($1)", [me.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    const targetId = (await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE LOWER(handle) = LOWER($1)", [target.replace('@', '')])).rows[0]?.id;
    
    if (!myId || !targetId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur introuvable dans la base' }, { status: 404 });

    await pool.query(`
      INSERT INTO friendships (requester_id, addressee_id, status) 
      VALUES ($1, $2, 'pending') ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    `, [myId, targetId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true });
  } catch (error: any) { 
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 }); 
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/github/sync/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { pubkey, github_username } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Sauvegarder le pseudo GitHub
    await pool.query("UPDATE users SET github_username = $1 WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $2", [github_username, pubkey]);
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    const userId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 2. Récupérer les derniers commits publics via l'API GitHub
    const ghRes = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${github_username}/events/public`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'SCEAU-App' }
    });
    const events = await ghRes.json();
    
    let commitsScelles = 0;
    for (const event of events) {
      if (event.type === 'PushEvent' && event.payload.commits.length > 0) {
        for (const commit of event.payload.commits) {
          // On crée un hash basé sur le message et l'ID du commit
          const hashStr = `${commit.sha}-${commit.message}`;
          const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(hashStr));
          let hashB64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...hash));

          // On insère dans la base (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING pour éviter les doublons)
          await pool.query(
            `INSERT INTO seals (hash, timestamp, author_id, title, category, work_type, is_public) 
             VALUES (decode($1, 'base64'), $2, $3, $4, 'Code', 'commit', true) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`,
            [hashB64, event.created_at, userId, `Commit: ${commit.message.slice(0, 50)}`]
          );
          commitsScelles++;
        }
      }
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, commitsScelles });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur GitHub' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { name, creator_pubkey } = await req.json();
  try {
    const creator = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [creator_pubkey]);
    if (creator.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const creatorId = creator.rows[0].id;

    const group = await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO groups (name, creator_id) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id",
      [name, creatorId]
    );
    const groupId = group.rows[0].id;

    // Le créateur est automatiquement membre
    await pool.query("INSERT INTO group_members (group_id, user_id) VALUES ($1, $2)", [groupId, creatorId]);
    
    return NextResponse.json({ group_id: groupId });
  } catch (error) { return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 }); }
}
