=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/verify/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, query } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Recherche par Hash (Vérification classique + Projets)
    if (hash) {
      // a) On cherche dans les sceaux individuels
      const sealResult = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, u.handle as author,
               'seal' as type
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE encode(s.hash, 'base64') = $1
      `, [hash]);

      // b) On cherche dans les fichiers de projets (Arbre de Merkle)
      const projectResult = await pool.query(`
        SELECT pf.file_name as title, p.created_at as timestamp, 
               CASE WHEN pf.file_path IS NOT NULL THEN true ELSE false END as is_hosted, 
               pf.file_path, 'Projet' as category, u.handle as author,
               'project' as type, p.title as project_title, p.id as project_id
        FROM project_files pf 
        JOIN projects p ON pf.project_id = p.id
        JOIN users u ON p.author_id = u.id
        WHERE pf.file_hash = $1
      `, [hash]);

      const combinedSeals = [...sealResult.rows, ...projectResult.rows];

      if (combinedSeals.length > 0) {
        return NextResponse.json({ found: true, seals: combinedSeals });
      }
      return NextResponse.json({ found: false });
    }

    // 2. Recherche par Titre (Découverte publique)
    if (query) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE s.title ILIKE $1 AND s.is_public = TRUE
        ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20
      `, [`%${query}%`]);
      
      return NextResponse.json({ found: result.rows.length > 0, seals: result.rows });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requête invalide' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/api/verify/timeline/route.ts
=========================================

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const start = searchParams.get('start');
  const end = searchParams.get('end');
  const isLive = searchParams.get('live') === 'true';

  try {
    let sql = `
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE
    `;
    const params: any[] = [];

    if (isLive) {
      // Mode direct : on prend les 20 derniers
      sql += ` ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20`;
    } else {
      // Mode période : filtré par dates
      if (start) {
        sql += ` AND s.timestamp >= $${params.length + 1}`;
        params.push(new Date(start));
      }
      if (end) {
        sql += ` AND s.timestamp <= $${params.length + 1}`;
        params.push(new Date(end));
      }
      sql += ` ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 50`;
    }

    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ timeline: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[timeline]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/archive/[id]/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function ArchiveDetail() {
  const params = useParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [site, setSite] = useState<any>(null);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);

  const loadArchive = async () => {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) { router.replace('/login'); return; }

    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/archive?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      const found = (d.archives || []).find((s: any) => s.main_id === params.id || s.pages.some((p: any) => p.id === params.id));
      if (found) {
        setSite(found);
        const page = found.pages.find((p: any) => p.id === params.id) || found.pages.find((p: any) => p.url === found.main_url) || found.pages[0];
        setCurrentPage(page);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadArchive(); }, [params.id]);

  async function runCheck() {
    if (!currentPage) return;
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return;

    setChecking(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/archive', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, url: currentPage.id, action: 'check' })
    });
    await res.json();
    await loadArchive();
    setChecking(false);
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;
  if (!site || !currentPage) return <div className="p-10 text-center">Archive introuvable.</div>;

  const daysSinceStart = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(site.created_at).getTime()) / 86400000);
  const isModified = currentPage.last_status === 'modified';
  const isError = currentPage.last_status === 'error';

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto font-sans w-full min-h-screen">
      <button onClick={() => router.push('/archive')} className="text-sm font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-muted)'}}>
        ← Retour à Archive
      </button>

      {/* Header site */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-6 mb-6 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 flex-wrap gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold font-serif mb-1 break-words" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
              {site.main_title}
            </h1>
            <a href={site.site_root} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-sm text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline break-all">
              {site.site_root}
            </a>
          </div>
          <div className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shrink-0 ${site.has_modifications ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-700'}`}>
            {site.has_modifications ? '⚠️ Modifié' : '✅ Intact'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">Suivi depuis</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{daysSinceStart} jour{daysSinceStart > 1 ? 's' : ''}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">Dernier scan</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{new Date(site.last_checked_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pages suivies</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{site.pages_count}</p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">Scans effectués</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{site.scan_count || 1}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Page courante */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)]">Page actuelle</p>
            <p className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
              {currentPage.title}
            </p>
            <a href={currentPage.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="text-xs text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline break-all">
              {currentPage.url}
            </a>
          </div>
          <div className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold shrink-0 ${isModified ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : isError ? 'bg-orange-50 text-orange-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-700'}`}>
            {isModified ? 'Modifié' : isError ? 'Inaccessible' : 'Intact'}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Statut / Diff */}
      {!isModified && !isError && (
        <div className="bg-[#F0FAF4] border border-[#A8C77E] rounded-2xl p-5 mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-[#3F8F5C] mb-2">✅ Aucun changement détecté</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#5D7A3A]">
            Le contenu de cette page est identique à la dernière vérification du {new Date(currentPage.last_checked_at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}.
            SCEAU surveille ce site en continu. Vous recevrez une alerte dès qu'un changement apparaîtra.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {isError && (
        <div className="bg-orange-50 border border-orange-200 rounded-2xl p-5 mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-orange-700 mb-2">🔒 Site inaccessible</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-orange-600">
            Le dernier scan a échoué (site temporairement inaccessible ou bloquant). SCEAU réessaiera automatiquement.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {isModified && currentPage.diff_data && (
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm mb-6">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>🔍 Changements détectés</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">{currentPage.diff_data.changesCount} ligne(s) modifiée(s)</p>
          </div>
          <div className="p-4 font-mono text-xs bg-[var(--bg-main)] max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
            {currentPage.diff_data.details?.map((line: any, i: number) => (
              <div key={i} className={`py-1 px-2 whitespace-pre-wrap ${
                line.type === 'added' ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-800 border-l-2 border-green-500' :
                line.type === 'removed' ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-800 border-l-2 border-red-500 line-through opacity-70' :
                'text-[var(--text-muted)]'
              }`}>
                <span className="opacity-50 mr-2">{line.type === 'added' ? '+' : line.type === 'removed' ? '-' : ' '}</span>
                {line.text || ' '}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Autres pages du site */}
      {site.pages.length > 1 && (
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm mb-3" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>📄 Autres pages suivies ({site.pages.length - 1})</h3>
          <div className="space-y-1 max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
            {site.pages.filter((p: any) => p.id !== currentPage.id).map((p: any) => (
              <Link 
                key={p.id} 
                href={`/archive/${p.id}`}
                className="block p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition text-xs"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{p.title}</span>
                  <span className={`shrink-0 text-[0.6rem] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${p.last_status === 'modified' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-700'}`}>
                    {p.last_status === 'modified' ? '⚠️' : '✅'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <button
          onClick={runCheck}
          disabled={checking}
          className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] bg-[#1A1513] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:bg-black transition disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {checking ? '⏳ Vérification...' : '🔄 Vérifier maintenant'}
        </button>
        <a
          href={currentPage.url}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] text-[var(--text-main)] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm text-center hover:border-[#E76F51] transition"
        >
          🌐 Visiter la page
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/archive/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function ArchivePage() {
  const [sites, setSites] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isPro, setIsPro] = useState(false);
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(5);
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const pubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const loadArchives = async () => {
    if (!pubkey) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/archive?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) {
        setSites(d.archives || []);
        setIsPro(d.is_pro);
        setLimit(d.limit || 5);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadArchives(); }, [pubkey]);

  async function handleAdd() {
    if (!url || !pubkey) return;
    setAdding(true); setError('');
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/archive', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, url })
      });
      const d = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok) setError(d.error || 'Erreur');
      else { setUrl(''); loadArchives(); }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError('Erreur réseau');
    }
    setAdding(false);
  }

  async function deleteSite(siteRoot: string, mainId: string) {
    if (!pubkey || !confirm('Supprimer ce site et toutes ses pages surveillées ?')) return;
    await fetch(`/api/archive?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}&id=${mainId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    loadArchives();
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto font-sans w-full min-h-screen">
      
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-8 flex-wrap gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold font-serif mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU Archive</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Surveillez l'intégrité des sites web. Détectez les modifications en un clic.</p>
        </div>
        <a 
          href="/downloads/sceau-extension.zip" 
          download 
          className="bg-white border-2 border-gray-200 text-gray-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs hover:border-[#E76F51] transition shadow-sm hidden md:block"
        >
          Télécharger l'Extension Chrome
        </a>
      </div>

      {/* Quota */}
      {!isPro && (
        <div className="bg-[#FDF4F0] border border-[#FCE0D6] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 mb-6 inline-block">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#C44A2E]">
            Quota Gratuit : {sites.length} / {limit} sites
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Formulaire */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mb-8">
        <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2 font-mono uppercase">
          Ajouter un site à surveiller
        </label>
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
          <input 
            type="url" 
            placeholder="https://www.exemple.com" 
            className="flex-1 min-w-0 border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-transparent outline-none focus:border-[#4A90C2]"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={url}
            onChange={e => setUrl(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && handleAdd()}
          />
          <button 
            onClick={handleAdd} 
            disabled={adding || (!isPro && sites.length >= limit)}
            className="bg-[#1A1513] text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-black transition whitespace-nowrap"
          >
            {adding ? '...' : 'Sceller'}
          </button>
        </div>
        {error && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm font-bold mt-2">{error}</p>}
        {!isPro && sites.length >= limit && (
          <p className="text-sm text-[#C44A2E] mt-3 font-bold">
            ⚠️ Limite de {limit} sites atteinte. <button onClick={() => alert("SCEAU PRO arrive bientôt !")} className="underline">Passez à PRO</button> pour en ajouter plus.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Liste groupée */}
      {loading ? (
        <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Chargement...</p>
      ) : sites.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-16 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl">
          <div className="text-5xl mb-4">📡</div>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Aucun site surveillé</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] max-w-sm mx-auto">
            Ajoutez l'URL d'un site pour que SCEAU le scanne chaque jour et vous alerte en cas de modification.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
          {sites.map((s, i) => (
            <div 
              key={s.site_root} 
              className={`flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between p-4 hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition ${i !== sites.length - 1 ? 'border-b border-[var(--border-color)]' : ''} ${s.has_modifications ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0]' : ''}`}
            >
              <Link href={`/archive/${s.main_id}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3 md:mb-0 flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${s.has_modifications ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-green-500'}`}></div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                    {s.main_title}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] truncate">
                    {s.site_root}
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                    <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                      {s.pages_count} page{s.pages_count > 1 ? 's' : ''}
                    </span>
                    {s.has_modifications && (
                      <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] border border-[#FCE0D6] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        Modifié
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                <span className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mr-2 hidden sm:block">
                  {new Date(s.last_checked_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
                </span>
                <Link
                  href={`/archive/${s.main_id}`}
                  className="bg-[#1A1513] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-black transition"
                >
                  Voir →
                </Link>
                <button 
                  onClick={() => deleteSite(s.site_root, s.main_id)} 
                  className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 px-2 text-lg"
                >
                  ✕
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/challenges/[id]/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function ChallengeDetail() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const load = () => {
    if (params.id) {
      fetch(`/api/challenges/${params.id}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => {
          if (d.error) setError(d.error);
          else setData(d);
        });
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [params.id]);

  async function vote(entryId: string) {
    if (!myPubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi pour voter !");
    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/vote', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ challenge_id: params.id, entry_id: entryId, pubkey: myPubkey }),
    });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (!res.ok) alert(d.error);
    else { alert('Vote enregistré !'); load(); }
  }

  if (error) return <div className="p-10 text-center text-red-500 font-bold">{error}</div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement du défi...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
      <Link href="/friends" className="text-sm font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-4 inline-block hover:underline">
        ← Retour aux défis
      </Link>

      <div className="text-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{data.challenge.title}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">{data.challenge.description}</p>
        <div className="mt-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">
          Fin le {new Date(data.challenge.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BOUTON PARTICIPER */}
      <div className="mb-8 text-center">
        <Link
          href={`/seal?challenge_id=${params.id}`}
          className="inline-block bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition"
        >
          ✍️ Participer — Sceller mon œuvre
        </Link>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mt-2">
          Ton œuvre sera scellée avant d'être soumise. Elle est protégée pendant le vote.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4">
        {data.entries.map((entry: any) => (
          <div key={entry.entry_id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-4 rounded-xl flex justify-between items-center shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{entry.title}</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{entry.author}</p>
              {entry.file_path && (
                <a href={entry.file_path} target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="text-xs text-[#4A90C2] font-bold hover:underline mt-2 block">
                  Voir l'œuvre ↗
                </a>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => vote(entry.entry_id)}
                className="bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] border border-[#F4D0A8] px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-[#FCE0D6] transition"
              >
                Voter
              </button>
              <div className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">{entry.votes} votes</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {data.entries.length === 0 && (
          <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Personne n'a encore participé. Sois le premier !</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/challenges/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Challenges() {
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [isAnon, setIsAnon] = useState(false);

  const load = () => {
    fetch('/api/challenges/list').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
      setChallenges(d.challenges || []);
      setLoading(false);
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);

  async function createChallenge() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi !");
    if (!title) return alert("Titre requis");

    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title, description: desc, pubkey, is_anonymous: isAnon })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      setShowModal(false); setTitle(''); setDesc(''); load();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>🏆 Défis de Preuve.</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setShowModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">+ Créer un défi</button>
      </div>

      {loading ? <p className="text-center font-mono">Chargement...</p> : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {challenges.map(c => (
            <Link href={`/challenges/${c.id}`} key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition block">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{c.title}</h2>
                {c.is_anonymous && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">Anonyme</span>}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Lancé par @{c.creator}</p>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono text-[#A89070]">
                <span>👥 {c.entries_count} participations</span>
                <span>⏱ Fin le {new Date(c.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {challenges.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucun défi en cours.</p>}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modale de création */}
      {showModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un Défi</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre (ex: Meilleur design de l'été)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={title} onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description du défi..." className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={desc} onChange={e => setDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isAnon} onChange={e => setIsAnon(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cacher les noms (Vote à l'aveugle)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createChallenge} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Lancer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/feed/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

function getFileType(url: string) {
  if (!url) return 'none';
  const ext = url.split('?')[0].split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
  if (['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp', 'svg'].includes(ext)) return 'image';
  if (['mp4', 'webm', 'mov'].includes(ext)) return 'video';
  if (['mp3', 'wav', 'ogg'].includes(ext)) return 'audio';
  if (ext === 'pdf') return 'pdf';
  return 'other';
}

export default function Feed() {
  const [seals, setSeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [stories, setStories] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedSeal, setSelectedSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all');
  const [activeStoryIndex, setActiveStoryIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [creations, setCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  
  const [showCreationsFeed, setShowCreationsFeed] = useState(false);
  const [globalCreations, setGlobalCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loadingGlobal, setLoadingGlobal] = useState(false);

  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = useState(false);
  const [createContent, setCreateContent] = useState('');
  const [createFile, setCreateFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);

  const myHandle = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') : null;
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const loadFeed = () => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/feed?me=${myHandle || ''}&category=${activeCategory}`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => { setSeals(data.seals || []); setLoading(false); });
  };

  const loadStories = () => {
    fetch('/api/feed/stories').then(res => res.json()).then(data => setStories(data.stories || []));
  };

  const loadCreations = (sealId: string) => {
    fetch(`/api/creations?seal_id=${sealId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setCreations(d.creations || []));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadFeed(); loadStories(); }, [myHandle, activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedSeal) loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }, [selectedSeal]);

  const loadGlobalFeed = () => {
    setLoadingGlobal(true);
    fetch('/api/creations/global')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setGlobalCreations(d.creations || []); setLoadingGlobal(false); })
      .catch(() => setLoadingGlobal(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showCreationsFeed) loadGlobalFeed();
  }, [showCreationsFeed]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeStoryIndex !== null) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (activeStoryIndex < stories.length - 1) setActiveStoryIndex(activeStoryIndex + 1);
        else setActiveStoryIndex(null);
      }, 5000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [activeStoryIndex, stories.length]);

  async function likeCreation(id: string) {
    if (!myPubkey) return alert('Connecte-toi pour liker !');
    await fetch('/api/creations/like', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ creation_id: id, pubkey: myPubkey })
    });
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
    if (showCreationsFeed) loadGlobalFeed();
  }

  async function submitCreation() {
    if ((!createContent.trim() && !createFile) || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsCreating(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', createContent);
    formData.append('type', createFile ? (createFile.type.startsWith('video') ? 'video' : 'image') : 'text');
    if (createFile) formData.append('file', createFile);

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    
    setCreateContent('');
    setCreateFile(null);
    setShowCreateModal(false);
    setIsCreating(false);
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }

  async function submitComment() {
    if (!newComment.trim() || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', newComment);
    formData.append('type', 'text');

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    setNewComment('');
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
    setIsSubmitting(false);
  }

  const rollRandom = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/feed/random');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.seal) {
      setSelectedSeal(data.seal);
      if (myPubkey) {
        fetch(`/api/seal/${data.seal.id}/log-view`, { 
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
          body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey: myPubkey, context: 'random' }) 
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    } else if (data.reason === 'empty') {
      alert("Le réseau est encore vide. Scelle la première œuvre !");
    } else {
      alert("Erreur réseau.");
    }
  };

  // === FEED GLOBAL (CRÉATIONS) ===
  if (showCreationsFeed) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-lg mx-auto font-sans min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(false)} className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            ← Retour
          </button>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Créations</h1>
        </div>

        {loadingGlobal ? (
           <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Chargement...</p>
        ) : globalCreations.length === 0 ? (
           <div className="text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
             <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucune création sur le réseau.</p>
           </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {globalCreations.map((c: any) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">
                      {c.author[0].toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] text-[var(--text-muted)]">a publié une création</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {c.media_url && (
                  <div className="w-full bg-black flex justify-center max-h-[400px] overflow-hidden">
                    {c.type === 'video' ? (
                      <video src={c.media_url} controls className="max-h-[400px] w-full object-contain" />
                    ) : (
                      <img src={c.media_url} alt="création" className="w-full object-cover max-h-[400px]" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="p-5">
                  {c.content && <p className="text-sm mb-4" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>}
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                    <button onClick={() => likeCreation(c.id)} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:scale-105 transition">
                      {c.likes_count > 0 ? c.likes_count : '0'} J'aime
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => { setShowCreationsFeed(false); setSelectedSeal({ id: c.seal_id, title: c.seal_title, author: c.author, description: '' }); }}
                    className="w-full text-left bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-[#E76F51] transition flex items-center justify-between group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Sceau fondateur</div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.seal_title}</div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[#E76F51] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // === FEED NORMAL (CADRANS) ===
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-6 font-sans max-w-3xl md:max-w-5xl mx-auto relative w-full">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold font-serif" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">.</span></h1>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Link href="/messages?pact=true" className="bg-[#1A1513] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:bg-black transition">
            + Pacte
          </Link>
          <Link href="/oracle" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">
            + Oracle
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 relative z-40">
        <input 
          type="text" 
          placeholder="Rechercher une œuvre ou un créateur..." 
          className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
          style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={async (e) => {
            setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
            if (e.target.value.length > 2) {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(e.target.value)}`);
              const d = await res.json();
              setSearchResults(d.results || []);
            } else {
              setSearchResults([]);
            }
          }}
        />
        {searchResults.length > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-full left-0 right-0 mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
             {searchResults.map(r => (
                <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { setSelectedSeal(r); setSearchResults([]); setSearchQuery(''); }} className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer">
                   <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{r.title}</div>
                   <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{r.author}</div>
                </div>
             ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {stories.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto pb-4 mb-2 snap-x hide-scrollbar">
          {stories.map((story, index) => (
            <div key={story.id} onClick={() => setActiveStoryIndex(index)} className="snap-start shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center cursor-pointer group">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full p-[2px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]">
                <div className="w-full h-full bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--bg-surface)] flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl overflow-hidden relative">
                  {story.file_path && story.is_hosted ? (
                    <img src={story.file_path} alt="story" className="w-full h-full object-cover" onError={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.display = 'none'; e.currentTarget.nextElementSibling?.classList.remove('hidden'); }} />
                  ) : null}
                  <span className="opacity-70 absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-[var(--bg-main)]" style={{ display: story.file_path && story.is_hosted ? 'none' : 'flex' }}>{story.author[0].toUpperCase()}</span>
                  <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-[0.5rem] px-1 rounded-tl-md">⏳</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mt-1 truncate w-16 text-center" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{story.author}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4">
        <button className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Cadrans (Œuvres)</button>
        <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(true)} className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg opacity-50">Créations (Feed)</button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 gap-2">
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 snap-x flex-1 hide-scrollbar">
          {['all', 'Art', 'Musique', 'Code', 'Écrit'].map(cat => (
            <button key={cat} onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)} className={`snap-start shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border ${activeCategory === cat ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white border-[#E76F51] shadow-md' : 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] text-[var(--text-muted)] border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
              {cat === 'all' ? 'Tout' : cat}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <button onClick={rollRandom} className="shrink-0 bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1">Aléatoire</button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
         <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Connexion...</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 mb-10">
          {seals.filter(s => !s.is_ephemeral).map((seal) => {
            const fileType = seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path ? getFileType(seal.file_path) : 'none';
            
            return (
            <div key={seal.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(seal)} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
              
              {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
                <div className="w-full h-48 bg-[var(--bg-main)] relative flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  {fileType === 'image' && <img src={seal.file_path} alt={seal.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />}
                  {fileType === 'video' && (
                    <div className="relative w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-black">
                      <video src={seal.file_path} className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-50" />
                      <div className="absolute text-white text-3xl opacity-80">▶</div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {fileType === 'pdf' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-[#E76F51]">
                      <div className="text-5xl mb-2">📄</div>
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--text-main)]">Document PDF</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {fileType === 'other' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-[#4A90C2]">
                      <div className="text-5xl mb-2">📎</div>
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--text-main)]">Fichier joint</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {fileType === 'audio' && (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-[#5D7A3A]">
                      <div className="text-5xl mb-2">🎵</div>
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-[var(--text-main)]">Fichier Audio</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h2>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2 font-mono">Par @{seal.author} • {seal.category}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic line-clamp-2 mt-auto">{seal.description}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )})}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === LE CADRAN CARRE === */}
      {selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-[var(--border-soft)]" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            
            {/* En-tête du Cadran */}
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-start bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-10">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.title}</h2>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{selectedSeal.author}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)} className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>

            {/* Contenu */}
            <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] space-y-4">
              
              {/* Bloc Description (Carré) */}
              <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                <h3 className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase mb-2 text-[var(--text-muted)] tracking-wider">Description du créateur</h3>
                <p className="text-sm italic" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.description || "Aucune description fournie."}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Bouton d'action (Créations) */}
              <button 
                onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#E76F51] bg-[#FDF4F0] hover:bg-[#FCEAD6] transition group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="text-2xl"></span>
                  <div className="text-left">
                    <div className="font-bold text-[#C44A2E]">
                      {creations.length > 0 ? `${creations.length} création(s) rattachée(s)` : "Aucune création"}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[#C44A2E] opacity-80">
                      {creations.length > 0 ? "Cliquez pour explorer" : "Lancez la vôtre !"}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="font-bold text-xl text-[#C44A2E] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
              </button>

              {/* Liste des créations (avec Likes) */}
              <div className="space-y-3 pb-4">
                {creations.map((c, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</span>
                      <span className="text-[0.6rem] bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded">{c.type === 'text' ? 'Texte' : c.type}</span>
                    </div>
                    {c.media_url && c.type !== 'text' && (
                      <div className="mb-3 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                        {c.type === 'video' ? <video src={c.media_url} controls className="w-full max-h-48 object-cover" /> : <img src={c.media_url} className="w-full max-h-48 object-cover" />}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <p className="text-sm mb-3" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>
                    <button onClick={() => likeCreation(c.id)} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#E76F51]">
                      {c.likes_count > 0 ? c.likes_count : '0'} J'aime
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Barre de commentaire (Fix Bouton) */}
            {myHandle && (
              <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]">
                <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={newComment} 
                    onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)} 
                    placeholder="Commenter..." 
                    className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#E76F51]" 
                    style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} 
                    onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && submitComment()}
                  />
                  <button 
                    onClick={submitComment} 
                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                    className="shrink-0 bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Publier
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === MODALE : CRÉER UNE CRÉATION === */}
      {showCreateModal && selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-xl p-6 shadow-2xl" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Nouvelle Création</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <textarea 
              className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mb-4" 
              rows={3} 
              placeholder="Description de ton média..."
              value={createContent}
              onChange={e => setCreateContent(e.target.value)}
              style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            />

            <div className="mb-6">
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" onChange={e => setCreateFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} className="w-full text-sm" />
            </div>

            <button onClick={submitCreation} disabled={isCreating} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md">
              {isCreating ? 'Publication...' : 'Publier'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </main>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/forgot-password/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [link, setLink] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    setLink(''); setError('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/forgot-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) setLink(data.link);
    else setError(data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4 font-serif">Mot de passe oublié</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-6">Entre ton email pour recevoir un lien de réinitialisation.</p>
      
      <input type="email" placeholder="Ton email" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold mb-4">Envoyer le lien</button>
      
      {link && (
        <div className="bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#7A9B5A] p-4 rounded-xl text-sm break-all">
          <p className="font-bold text-[#5D7A3A] mb-2">✅ (Mode Test) Voici ton lien :</p>
          <a href={link} className="text-blue-600 underline">{link}</a>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500 font-bold text-center">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/friends/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Friends() {
  // État pour basculer entre "Amis" et "Défis"
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'network' | 'challenges'>('network');

  // États pour les Amis
  const [friendsData, setFriendsData] = useState<any>({ friends: [], received: [] });
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendsLoading, setFriendsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [friendsError, setFriendsError] = useState('');
  
  // États pour les Défis
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [challengesLoading, setChallengesLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showChallengeModal, setShowChallengeModal] = useState(false);
  const [challengeTitle, setChallengeTitle] = useState('');
  const [challengeDesc, setChallengeDesc] = useState('');
  const [challengeAnon, setChallengeAnon] = useState(false);

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h ? h.replace('@', '') : null);
  }, []);

  // --- LOGIQUE AMIS ---
  const loadFriends = () => {
    if (!myHandle) return;
    setFriendsLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}`)
      .then(async (r) => {
        const d = await r.json();
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'Erreur de chargement');
        return d;
      })
      .then(d => { 
        setFriendsData({ friends: d.friends || [], received: d.received || [] }); 
        setFriendsLoading(false);
      })
      .catch((err) => { 
        setFriendsError(err.message); 
        setFriendsLoading(false); 
      });
  };

  useEffect(() => { 
    if (myHandle && activeTab === 'network') loadFriends(); 
  }, [myHandle, activeTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim() || !myHandle) { setSearchResults([]); return; }
    const delay = setTimeout(() => {
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}&search=${searchQuery}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setSearchResults(d.searchResults || []))
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(delay);
  }, [searchQuery, myHandle]);

  async function respond(target: string, action: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends/respond', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target, action }) });
    loadFriends();
  }

  async function addFriend(target: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target }) });
    alert(`Demande envoyée à @${target}`);
    setSearchQuery('');
  }

  // --- LOGIQUE DÉFIS ---
  const loadChallenges = () => {
    setChallengesLoading(true);
    fetch('/api/challenges/list')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setChallenges(d.challenges || []);
        setChallengesLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setChallengesLoading(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'challenges') loadChallenges();
  }, [activeTab]);

  async function createChallenge() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi !");
    if (!challengeTitle) return alert("Titre requis");

    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title: challengeTitle, description: challengeDesc, pubkey, is_anonymous: challengeAnon })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      setShowChallengeModal(false); 
      setChallengeTitle(''); 
      setChallengeDesc(''); 
      loadChallenges();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif min-h-screen">
      
      {/* EN-TÊTE ET BOUTONS DE NAVIGATION */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Communauté.</h1>
        
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('network')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'network' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            👥 Le Réseau
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('challenges')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'challenges' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            🏆 Les Défis
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : RÉSEAU (AMIS) */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'network' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          {friendsError ? (
            <div className="text-center p-10 font-mono">
              <p className="text-red-500 font-bold mb-4">❌ {friendsError}</p>
              <button onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/login'; }} className="bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Se reconnecter</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-8 relative">
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="🔍 Chercher un créateur par pseudo..." 
                  className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
                  style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                />
                {searchResults.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg absolute w-full z-10">
                    {searchResults.map(u => (
                      <div key={u.handle} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                        <div>
                          <Link href={`/u/${u.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{u.handle}</Link>
                          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] ml-2">{u.role}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button onClick={() => addFriend(u.handle)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">+ Ajouter</button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {friendsData.received.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-8">
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#E76F51] mb-3">Demandes en attente ({friendsData.received.length})</h2>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {friendsData.received.map((r: any) => (
                      <div key={r.handle} className="bg-[#FDF4F0] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#FCE0D6] flex justify-between items-center">
                        <Link href={`/u/${r.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{r.handle}</Link>
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'accept')} className="bg-green-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Accepter</button>
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'reject')} className="bg-red-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Refuser</button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3">Mes Amis ({friendsData.friends.length})</h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {friendsData.friends.map((f: any) => (
                  <div key={f.handle} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                      <Link href={`/u/${f.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{f.handle}</Link>
                      <span className="text-xs bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded">Ami</span>
                    </div>
                    {f.last_seal ? (
                      <Link href={`/o/${f.last_seal_id}`} className="block text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                        Dernière œuvre : <span className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{f.last_seal}"</span>
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune œuvre publique récente.</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {friendsData.friends.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic col-span-2 text-center py-4">Ta communauté est vide. Cherche des créateurs ci-dessus !</p>}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : DÉFIS */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'challenges' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Lancez des duels de créativité, votez pour le meilleur.</p>
            <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md whitespace-nowrap">+ Créer un défi</button>
          </div>

          {challengesLoading ? <p className="text-center font-mono">Chargement...</p> : (
            <div className="grid gap-4">
              {challenges.map(c => (
                <Link href={`/challenges/${c.id}`} key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition block">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{c.title}</h2>
                    {c.is_anonymous && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">Vote à l'aveugle</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Lancé par @{c.creator}</p>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono text-[#A89070]">
                    <span>👥 {c.entries_count} participations</span>
                    <span>⏱ Fin le {new Date(c.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
              {challenges.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucun défi en cours. Sois le premier à en lancer un !</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE DE CRÉATION DE DÉFI */}
      {showChallengeModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un Défi</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre (ex: Meilleur design de l'été)" className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeTitle} onChange={e => setChallengeTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description du défi..." className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeDesc} onChange={e => setChallengeDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={challengeAnon} onChange={e => setChallengeAnon(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cacher les noms (Vote à l'aveugle)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createChallenge} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Lancer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

@import "tailwindcss";

@theme {
  --font-sans: 'Outfit', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-serif: 'Instrument Serif', Georgia, serif;
  --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
}

@layer base {
  :root {
    --bg-main: #F9F6F0;
    --bg-surface: #FFFFFF;
    --bg-soft: #FDF8F0;
    --text-main: #2C1810;
    --text-muted: #8A7A72;
    --border-color: #EFE0CA;
    --border-soft: #F5E9D8;
    --shadow-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(44,24,16,0.04);
    --shadow-md: 0 4px 12px rgba(44,24,16,0.06);
    --radius-card: 16px;
  }
  
  .dark {
    --bg-main: #0F0F0F; /* Noir très profond */
    --bg-surface: #1A1A1A; /* Gris foncé pour les cartes */
    --text-main: #EDEDED;
    --text-muted: #A0A0A0;
    --border-color: #2A2A2A;
  }
  
  /* On force la Sidebar à rester sombre même en mode clair */
  .dark aside, .dark .Cockpit {
    background-color: #050505 !important;
    border-color: #2A2A2A !important;
  }

  body {
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    color: var(--text-main);
    transition: background-color 0.3s, color 0.3s;
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }

  /* Adoucir les titres par défaut */
  h1, h2, h3, h4 {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  }

  /* Utiliser la Serif uniquement pour les moments forts */
  .font-serif {
    font-family: var(--font-serif) !important;
    font-weight: 400 !important;
    letter-spacing: 0 !important;
  }
}

@keyframes progress {
  from { width: 0%; }
  to { width: 100%; }
}

.hide-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar {
  display: none;
}
.hide-scrollbar {
  -ms-overflow-style: none;
  scrollbar-width: none;
}


@keyframes slide-in {
  from { transform: translateX(-100%); opacity: 0; }
  to { transform: translateX(0); opacity: 1; }
}
.animate-slide-in {
  animation: slide-in 0.25s ease-out forwards;
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/layout.tsx
