'use client';
import './globals.css'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import Cockpit from '@/components/Cockpit'

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0);
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js').catch(err => console.log('SW registration failed:', err));
    }
    setHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'));
    const theme = localStorage.getItem('sceau_theme');
    if (theme === 'dark') { setIsDark(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, [pathname]);

  // Fermer le menu mobile au changement de page
  useEffect(() => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(false);
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!handle) return;
    const fetchNotifs = () => {
      fetch(`/api/notifications?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        setNotifications(d.notifications || []);
        setUnread(d.unread || 0);
      });
    };
    fetchNotifs();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchNotifs, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [handle]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = !isDark; setIsDark(next);
    if (next) { document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'light'); }
  };

  const isActive = (path: string) => pathname === path;
  const isToolsActive = pathname === '/seal' || pathname === '/verify';

  const navSections = [
    {
      title: 'Espace Public',
      links: [
        { name: 'Le Flux', path: '/feed' },
        { name: "L'Atelier", path: '/seal' },
      ]
    },
    {
      title: 'Espace Pro',
      links: [
        { name: 'SCEAU Projets', path: '/projets' },
        { name: 'SCEAU Archive', path: '/archive' },
        { name: 'SCEAU Oracle', path: '/oracle' },
        { name: 'Mon Profil', path: '/profil' },
        { name: 'Vérification', path: '/verify' },
        { name: 'Messagerie & Pactes', path: '/messages' },
      ]
    },
    {
      title: 'Réseau',
      links: [
        { name: 'Communauté', path: '/friends' },
        { name: 'Paramètres', path: '/settings' },
      ]
    }
  ];

  return (
    <html lang="fr">
      <head>
        <link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.json" />
        <meta name="theme-color" content="#E76F51" />
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Instrument+Serif:ital@0;1&family=Outfit:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
      </head>
      
      <body className="h-screen overflow-hidden flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] font-sans">
        
        {/* SIDEBAR GAUCHE - Desktop */}
        <aside className="hidden md:flex flex-col w-64 bg-[#2C1810] text-[#F5E9D8] border-r border-[#3D2F28] shrink-0">
          <div className="p-8 pb-4">
            <Link href="/feed" className="text-3xl font-bold font-serif text-white tracking-wide">SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
            <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono tracking-widest uppercase mt-2 text-[#8A7A72]">Notaire de Poche</div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-6 px-4">
            {navSections.map(section => (
              <div key={section.title} className="mb-8">
                <div className="px-4 text-[0.65rem] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#A89070] mb-3">{section.title}</div>
                {section.links.map(link => (
                  <Link key={link.path} href={link.path} className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors border-l-2 mb-1 ${isActive(link.path) ? 'bg-white/10 text-white border-[#E76F51]' : 'border-transparent text-[#EADFD4] hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-white'}`}>
                    {link.name}
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="p-6 border-t border-[#332824]">
            <Link href="/profil" className="flex items-center gap-3 hover:opacity-80 transition group">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs shadow-sm">
                {handle ? handle[0].toUpperCase() : '?'}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-[#E76F51] transition-colors">@{handle || 'Invité'}</p>
                <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[#8A7A72]">Paramètres du compte</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </aside>

        {/* CONTENEUR PRINCIPAL */}
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden relative">
          
          {/* NAV TOP MOBILE */}
          <nav className="md:hidden bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center shrink-0">
            <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="text-lg" title="Thème">{isDark ? '☀️' : '🌙'}</button>
              <button onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(true)} className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} title="Menu">☰</button>
            </div>
          </nav>

          {/* ZONE DE CONTENU */}
          <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto w-full pb-32 md:pb-0">
             <div className="w-full h-full">
               {children}
             </div>
          </main>

          {/* NAV BOTTOM MOBILE - LES 5 ESSENTIELS */}
          <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-around py-2 z-50 text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono">
            <Link href="/feed" className={`flex flex-col items-center px-2 py-1 ${isActive('/feed') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)]'}`}>
              <span className="text-lg">🌐</span>
              <span className="mt-0.5">Flux</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/projets" className={`flex flex-col items-center px-2 py-1 ${isActive('/projets') || pathname?.startsWith('/projets/') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)]'}`}>
              <span className="text-lg">📁</span>
              <span className="mt-0.5">Projets</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/seal" className={`flex flex-col items-center px-2 py-1 ${isToolsActive ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)]'}`}>
              <span className="text-lg">✍️</span>
              <span className="mt-0.5">Atelier</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/archive" className={`flex flex-col items-center px-2 py-1 ${isActive('/archive') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)]'}`}>
              <span className="text-lg">📡</span>
              <span className="mt-0.5">Archive</span>
            </Link>
            <Link href="/profil" className={`flex flex-col items-center px-2 py-1 ${isActive('/profil') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)]'}`}>
              <span className="text-lg">👤</span>
              <span className="mt-0.5">Profil</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* COCKPIT - Desktop seulement */}
        <Cockpit />

        {/* DRAWER MOBILE - Menu burger */}
        {mobileMenuOpen && (
          <div className="md:hidden fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[100] backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
            <div 
              className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-80 max-w-[85vw] h-full p-6 flex flex-col shadow-2xl animate-slide-in" 
              onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
                <button onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)} className="text-2xl opacity-60" title="Fermer">✕</button>
              </div>

              {handle && (
                <Link 
                  href="/profil" 
                  onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)} 
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 p-3 bg-[var(--bg-main)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]"
                >
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold">
                    {handle[0].toUpperCase()}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{handle}</p>
                    <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[var(--text-muted)]">Voir mon profil</p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[var(--text-muted)]">→</span>
                </Link>
              )}

              <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto -mx-2 px-2">
                {navSections.map(section => (
                  <div key={section.title} className="mb-4">
                    <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2 px-3">{section.title}</p>
                    {section.links.map(link => (
                      <Link 
                        key={link.path} 
                        href={link.path}
                        className={`block py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg mb-0.5 transition ${isActive(link.path) ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] font-bold' : 'hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)]'}`}
                        style={{color: isActive(link.path) ? '#C44A2E' : 'var(--text-main)'}}
                      >
                        {link.name}
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </nav>

              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-2">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/login'; }}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg border border-[#D64545] text-[#D64545] font-bold text-sm hover:bg-[#FDF4F0] transition"
                >
                  Se déconnecter
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/login/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) return setStatus("Email et mot de passe requis");
    setStatus("Connexion...");
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', data.pubkey);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.handle);
      setStatus("✅ Connecté !");
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
    } else {
      setStatus("❌ " + data.error);
    }
  }

  // La fonction magique pour "Entrée"
  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLogin(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Se connecter</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-2">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "🙈" : "👁️"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mb-6">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-sm text-[#E76F51] hover:underline font-bold">Mot de passe oublié ?</Link>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Connexion</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pas de compte ? <Link href="/signup" className="underline font-bold">Créer un compte</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/messages/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { generatePactCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

function fireConfetti() {
  const colors = ['#E76F51', '#F4A261', '#E9C46A', '#2A9D8F', '#4A90C2'];
  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.style.position = 'fixed'; el.style.width = '10px'; el.style.height = '10px';
    el.style.backgroundColor = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];
    el.style.left = Math.random() * 100 + 'vw'; el.style.top = '-10px';
    el.style.borderRadius = Math.random() > 0.5 ? '50%' : '0';
    el.style.zIndex = '9999'; el.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    el.style.transition = `transform ${1 + Math.random() * 2}s ease-in, opacity ${1 + Math.random()}s ease-out`;
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      el.style.transform = `translateY(${window.innerHeight + 100}px) rotate(${Math.random() * 360}deg)`;
      el.style.opacity = '0';
    });
    setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3000);
  }
}

function PactMessage({ pactId, onOpen }: { pactId: string, onOpen: (id: string) => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
      if (!d.error) setPact(d);
    });
  }, [pactId]);

  if (!pact) return <div className="p-4 bg-[var(--bg-main)] rounded-xl text-center text-xs font-mono my-2 border border-[var(--border-color)]" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Chargement du pacte...</div>;

  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';
  const progress = Math.min(100, ((pact.signataires?.length || 0) / pact.required_signatures) * 100);

  return (
    <div className={`w-full max-w-md bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-2 rounded-xl p-4 my-2 shadow-sm ${isSealed ? 'border-[#E9C46A]' : 'border-[#4A90C2]'}`}>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
         <div>
           <h3 className="font-bold text-lg leading-tight" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{pact.title}</h3>
           <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pacte de @{pact.creator}</p>
         </div>
         {isSealed && <span className="bg-[#E9C46A] text-[#2C1810] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.6rem] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Scellé</span>}
      </div>
      
      {pact.description && <p className="text-sm mb-3 italic" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>"{pact.description}"</p>}

      {/* Fichiers */}
      {pact.media_urls && pact.media_urls.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-4 space-y-1">
          <p className="text-[0.65rem] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Documents joints</p>
          {pact.media_urls.map((url: string, i: number) => (
             <a key={i} href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline bg-[#F0F6FC] p-2 rounded border border-[#C9D8E8]">
                Pièce jointe {i+1}
             </a>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Signatures */}
      <div className="mb-4">
         <div className="flex justify-between text-[0.65rem] font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>
           <span>Signatures</span>
           <span>{pact.signataires?.length || 0} / {pact.required_signatures}</span>
         </div>
         <div className="w-full bg-[var(--border-color)] rounded-full h-1.5 mb-2">
            <div className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ${isSealed ? 'bg-[#E9C46A]' : 'bg-[#4A90C2]'}`} style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}></div>
         </div>
         <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
            {pact.signataires?.map((s: string) => (
              <span key={s} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)] text-[0.65rem] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)]">@{s}</span>
            ))}
         </div>
      </div>

      <button onClick={() => onOpen(pactId)} className={`w-full py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold transition ${isSealed ? 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)] hover:opacity-80' : 'bg-[#4A90C2] text-white hover:bg-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>
        {isSealed ? 'Voir le certificat' : 'Ouvrir / Signer'}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function MessagesInner() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const targetUser = searchParams.get('user');

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState('');
  const [myPubkey, setMyPubkey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendHandles, setFriendHandles] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [activeChat, setActiveChat] = useState<any>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');

  const [showGroupModal, setShowGroupModal] = useState(false);
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [activePact, setActivePact] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSealingChat, setIsSealingChat] = useState(false);
  const [showSharedVault, setShowSharedVault] = useState(false);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recordingTime, setRecordingTime] = useState(0);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const recordingIntervalRef = useRef<any>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMessagesLength = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') || '';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setMyHandle(handle);
    setMyPubkey(pubkey);

    if (handle) {
      fetch(`/api/messages?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setContacts(d.contacts || []));
      fetch(`/api/groups/list?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setGroups(d.groups || []));
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        const handles = new Set<string>((d.friends || []).map((f: any) => f.handle));
        setFriendHandles(handles);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (targetUser) setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: targetUser, name: targetUser });
  }, [targetUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChat || !myHandle) return;
    const load = () => {
      const endpoint = activeChat.type === 'dm'
        ? `/api/messages/${activeChat.id}?me=${myHandle}`
        : `/api/groups/messages?id=${activeChat.id}`;

      fetch(endpoint).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        const serverMessages = data.messages || [];
        setMessages(prev => {
          const tempMessages = prev.filter(m => m.isTemp);
          const merged = [...serverMessages];
          tempMessages.forEach(temp => {
            if (!serverMessages.some((sm: any) => sm.content === temp.content && sm.sender === temp.sender)) merged.push(temp);
          });
          return merged;
        });
      });
    };
    load();
    const interval = setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [activeChat, myHandle]);

  useEffect(() => { prevMessagesLength.current = 0; }, [activeChat]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (messages.length > prevMessagesLength.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      prevMessagesLength.current = messages.length;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !activeChat) return;
    const msg = newMessage;
    setNewMessage('');
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { content: msg, sender: myHandle, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), isTemp: true, id: tempId }]);

    try {
      if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
        await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg }) });
      } else {
        await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg }) });
      }
      setTimeout(() => setMessages(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== tempId)), 500);
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  }

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      setRecordingTime(0);
      recordingIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => setRecordingTime(t => t + 1), 1000);

      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
        if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data);
      };

      mediaRecorder.onstop = async () => {
        clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        if (audioBlob.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return alert("Audio trop lourd (max 2 Mo).");
        if (!myPubkey) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
        formData.append('audio', new File([audioBlob], `voice-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));

        const res = await fetch('/api/messages/upload-audio', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !data.url) return alert('Erreur upload audio');

        const msg = `[AUDIO]${data.url}`;
        if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        }
      };

      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch (err) { alert("Impossible d'accéder au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
      clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
    }
  }

  async function sealDiscussion() {
    if (!messages.length || !myPubkey) return;
    const confirm = window.confirm(`Sceller les ${Math.min(messages.length, 50)} derniers messages dans ton Coffre Privé ?`);
    if (!confirm) return;

    setIsSealingChat(true);
    const lastMessages = messages.slice(-50);
    const textToSeal = lastMessages.map(m => `[@${m.sender} - ${new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString()}]\n${m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]') ? 'Message vocal' : m.content}`).join('\n\n');

    const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textToSeal));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', btoa(hashStr));
    formData.append('title', `Discussion avec ${activeChat.name} — ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    formData.append('description', textToSeal);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('visibility', 'private');
    formData.append('isHosted', 'false');
    formData.append('isFlash', 'false');
    formData.append('isMystery', 'false');

    const res = await fetch('/api/seal', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    if (res.ok) alert("✅ Discussion scellée ! Retrouve-la dans ton Coffre Privé.");
    else alert("Erreur lors du scellement.");
    setIsSealingChat(false);
  }

  async function createGroup() {
    if (!groupName || !myPubkey) return;
    const res = await fetch('/api/groups/create', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: groupName, creator_pubkey: myPubkey }) });
    if (res.ok) { alert("Groupe créé !"); setShowGroupModal(false); window.location.reload(); }
  }

  const startPact = () => router.push(`/seal?pact=true&chat_id=${activeChat.id}&chat_type=${activeChat.type}`);

  const getHandleColor = (handle: string) => {
    if (handle === myHandle) return 'var(--text-main)';
    return friendHandles.has(handle) ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22';
  };

  const sharedPacts = Array.from(
    new Map(
      messages
        .filter(m => m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]'))
        .map(m => {
          const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
          return [id, { id, title }];
        })
    ).values()
  );

  const activeContacts = contacts.filter(c => c.has_chat);
  const suggestions = contacts.filter(c => !c.has_chat);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 flex gap-4 font-serif w-full max-w-5xl md:max-w-none mx-auto relative h-full">
      {/* LISTE */}
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 overflow-y-auto ${activeChat ? 'hidden md:block md:w-1/3' : 'w-full md:w-1/3'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Messages</h1>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(true)} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">Groupe</button>
            <button onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: 'nouveau', name: 'Nouveau contact' })} className="bg-[#1A1513] text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-black transition">+ Pacte</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {groups.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Groupes</h2>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <div key={g.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'group', id: g.id, name: g.name })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#2C1810] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">G</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{g.name}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Discussions</h2>
        {activeContacts.map(c => {
          const isFriend = friendHandles.has(c.handle);
          return (
            <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-white" style={{ background: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22' }}>
                {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22'}}>@{c.handle}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
        {activeContacts.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Aucune discussion active.</p>}

        <div className="mt-6 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-4">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Suggestions</h2>
          {suggestions.length > 0 ? (
            suggestions.map(c => (
              <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 opacity-70 hover:opacity-100">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">@{c.handle}</div>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune suggestion pour le moment.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CHAT */}
      {activeChat ? (
        <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden w-full md:w-2/3 relative`}>
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveChat(null)} className="md:hidden text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>←</button>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {activeChat.type === 'group' ? `👥 ${activeChat.name}` : `@${activeChat.name}`}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(true)} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">Coffre</button>
              <button onClick={sealDiscussion} disabled={isSealingChat} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg hover:border-[#E76F51] transition disabled:opacity-50">{isSealingChat ? '...' : 'Sceller'}</button>
              <button onClick={startPact} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg hover:bg-[#2D6A9E] transition">+ Pacte</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]')) {
                const [id] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
                return <PactMessage key={i} pactId={id} onOpen={setActivePact} />;
              }

              const isAudio = m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]');
              const audioSrc = isAudio ? m.content.replace('[AUDIO]', '') : null;

              return (
                <div key={i} className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${m.sender === myHandle ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white ml-auto rounded-br-none' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-none'} ${m.isTemp ? 'opacity-60' : ''}`}>
                  {activeChat.type === 'group' && m.sender !== myHandle && (
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: getHandleColor(m.sender) }}>@{m.sender}</div>
                  )}
                  {isAudio ? <audio controls src={audioSrc!} className="h-10 w-56" /> : m.content}
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex gap-2 items-center">
            <button onClick={isRecording ? stopRecording : startRecording} className={`p-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)]'}`} style={{ color: isRecording ? 'white' : 'var(--text-main)' }}>
              {isRecording ? `${recordingTime}s` : 'M'}
            </button>
            <input type="text" value={newMessage} onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="Écrire..." className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#E76F51]" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
            <button onClick={sendMessage} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Envoyer</button>
          </div>

          {showSharedVault && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-20 flex flex-col rounded-2xl">
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Coffre Partagé</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50">✕</button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Retrouvez ici tous les pactes échangés dans cette discussion.</p>
                {sharedPacts.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-center text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucun pacte pour le moment.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {sharedPacts.map(p => (
                      <div key={p.id} onClick={() => { setShowSharedVault(false); setActivePact(p.id); }} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-4 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{p.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Cliquez pour voir les détails et signer</div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          Sélectionnez une discussion.
        </div>
      )}

      {showGroupModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un Groupe</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Nom du groupe" className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={groupName} onChange={e => setGroupName(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createGroup} className="flex-1 bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Créer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activePact && <PactModal pactId={activePact} onClose={() => setActivePact(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PactModal({ pactId, onClose }: { pactId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isDownloading, setIsDownloading] = useState(false);
  const [isSigning, setIsSigning] = useState(false);
  const [justSealed, setJustSealed] = useState(false);
  
  const loadPact = () => fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setPact(d));
  useEffect(() => { loadPact(); }, [pactId]);

  async function signPact() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi.");
    
    setIsSigning(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/pacts/sign', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ pact_id: pactId, pubkey }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (data.success) {
      if (data.isNowSealed) {
        fireConfetti();
        setJustSealed(true);
      }
      loadPact();
    } else {
      alert(data.error);
    }
    setIsSigning(false);
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!pact) return;
    setIsDownloading(true);
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${pact.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generatePactCertificate(
      pact.title, pact.description, pact.creator, pact.signataires || [], 
      pact.hash_str || 'Hash en cours de calcul', qrDataUrl, pact.id
    );
    setIsDownloading(false);
  }

  if (!pact) return null;
  const myHandle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
  const hasSigned = pact.signataires?.includes(myHandle);
  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';

  if (justSealed) {
    return (
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
        <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-8 border-4 border-[#E9C46A] shadow-2xl relative text-center">
           <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🏆</div>
           <h2 className="text-3xl font-serif font-bold text-[#2C1810] mb-2">Pacte Scellé !</h2>
           <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-6">L'empreinte de ce contrat a été gravée sur Bitcoin pour l'éternité.</p>
           
           <div className="space-y-3">
             <a href={`/api/pacts/${pact.id}/ots`} download className="w-full block bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#4A6330] transition">
               Télécharger la preuve .ots
             </a>
             <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={isDownloading} className="w-full bg-[#E9C46A] text-[#2C1810] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4D03F] transition">
               {isDownloading ? 'Génération...' : 'Télécharger le Certificat PDF'}
             </button>
             <button onClick={onClose} className="w-full py-2 font-bold text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 transition">
               Fermer
             </button>
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const progress = Math.min(100, ((pact.signataires?.length || 0) / pact.required_signatures) * 100);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/70 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div className={`bg-white w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl border shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto ${isSealed ? 'border-[#E9C46A]' : 'border-[#E5E5E5]'}`}>
        
        {isSealed && (
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full bg-[#E9C46A] text-[#2C1810] py-1.5 rounded-t-2xl text-center text-xs font-bold tracking-widest uppercase">
            Contrat Scellé sur Bitcoin
          </div>
        )}

        <div className={`p-6 ${isSealed ? 'mt-6' : ''}`}>
          <div className="mb-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-serif font-bold text-gray-900 leading-tight">{pact.title}</h2>
            <p className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-500 mt-1">Proposé par @{pact.creator}</p>
          </div>

          {pact.description && (
            <div className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-100 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed">"{pact.description}"</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {pact.media_urls && pact.media_urls.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Documents du contrat</h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {pact.media_urls.map((url: string, i: number) => (
                  <a key={i} href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-black bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 hover:border-gray-400 transition">
                    <span>Document {i + 1}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-blue-600 font-bold">Télécharger</span>
                  </a>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mb-8">
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-2">
              <span>Signatures ({pact.signataires?.length || 0} / {pact.required_signatures})</span>
              {isSealed && <span className="text-green-600 uppercase tracking-wide">Validé</span>}
            </div>
            <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-1.5 mb-4">
              <div className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ${isSealed ? 'bg-[#E9C46A]' : 'bg-black'}`} style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}></div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {(pact.signataires || []).map((s: string) => (
                <span key={s} className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border border-gray-200 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-medium">@{s}</span>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {isSealed ? (
              <>
                <div className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 p-3 rounded-lg text-center mb-2">
                  <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">Empreinte SHA-256</p>
                  <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-gray-800 break-all">{pact.hash_str}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={isDownloading} className="w-full bg-black text-white py-3.5 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition">
                  {isDownloading ? 'Génération...' : 'Télécharger le Certificat PDF'}
                </button>
                <a href={`/api/pacts/${pact.id}/ots`} download className="w-full block text-center bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white py-3.5 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-[#4A6330] transition">
                  Télécharger la preuve .ots
                </a>
              </>
            ) : (
              <button onClick={signPact} disabled={hasSigned || isSigning} className={`w-full py-3.5 rounded-xl font-bold text-white transition ${hasSigned ? 'bg-green-600 cursor-default' : 'bg-black hover:bg-gray-800'}`}>
                {isSigning ? 'Ancrage en cours...' : hasSigned ? 'Vous avez signé' : 'Contresigner le contrat'}
              </button>
            )}
            <button onClick={onClose} className="w-full py-2 text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-800 transition">
              Fermer
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Messages() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}>
      <MessagesInner />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

'use client';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-6xl mb-6">🔍</div>
      <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Œuvre introuvable.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-8 max-w-sm mx-auto">
        Cette page n'existe pas, ou l'œuvre a été déplacée dans le coffre privé de son créateur.
      </p>
      <Link href="/feed" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/o/[id]/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generateCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

export default function PublicSealPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [seal, setSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshMsg, setRefreshMsg] = useState('');
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentVersion, setCurrentVersion] = useState(1);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!params.id) return;

    fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/versions`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setVersions(d.versions || []);
        setCurrentVersion(d.current_version || 1);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    fetch(`/api/o/${params.id}${qs}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.seal) setSeal(d.seal);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [params.id]);

  async function refreshOts() {
    setRefreshMsg('Interrogation des calendars Blockchain...');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/ots/refresh`, { method: 'POST' });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.status === 'upgraded') setRefreshMsg('✅ Preuve upgradée avec confirmation Blockchain');
    else setRefreshMsg(d.message || '⏳ Pas encore confirmé.');
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!seal) return;
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${seal.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generateCertificate(
      seal.title,
      seal.author,
      seal.timestamp,
      seal.hash,
      qrDataUrl,
      seal.hash_hex,
      seal.id
    );
  }

  function downloadUrl() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return '#';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    return `/api/download/${params.id}${qs}`;
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;
  if (!seal) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">Œuvre introuvable.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 font-serif">
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]"></div>

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <span className="inline-block bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#A89070] text-xs font-mono font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full italic mb-4">
            Preuve d'antériorité
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h1>
          <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)]">
            Créé par{' '}
            <Link href={`/u/${seal.author}`} className="font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:underline">
              @{seal.author}
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>

        {seal.description && (
          <p className="text-center italic mb-6 opacity-80" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            "{seal.description}"
          </p>
        )}

        {versions.length > 1 && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
              <span className="text-xl">📚</span>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E]">Dossier Miroir</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E]">{versions.length} versions — celle-ci est la V{currentVersion}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {versions.map((v: any) => (
                <a
                  key={v.id}
                  href={`/o/${v.id}`}
                  className={`flex justify-between items-center p-2 rounded-lg text-xs ${v.id === seal.id ? 'bg-white border border-[#4A90C2] font-bold' : 'hover:bg-white'}`}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>V{v.version_number} · {v.title}</span>
                  <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[#2D6A9E]">
                    {new Date(v.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} {v.has_ots && '⛓️'}
                  </span>
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] mb-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">SHA-256</p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono break-all" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            {seal.hash_hex || seal.hash}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">
            Scellé le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'border-[#7A9B5A] bg-[#F5F9EE]' : 'border-[#F4D0A8] bg-[#FCEAD6]'}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <span className="text-2xl">⛓️</span>
            <div>
              <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                Ancrage Blockchain (OpenTimestamps)
              </p>
              <p className={`text-xs ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#7A9B5A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                {seal.has_ots_proof ? 'Preuve .ots disponible' : 'Indisponible'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {seal.has_ots_proof && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
              <a href={`/api/seal/${seal.id}/ots`} download className="bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                ⬇️ Télécharger .ots
              </a>
              <button onClick={refreshOts} className="bg-white border-2 border-[#7A9B5A] text-[#5D7A3A] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                🔄 Vérifier confirmation
              </button>
              <a href="https://opentimestamps.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="bg-white border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] text-[var(--text-main)] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                🔍 Vérifier en ligne
              </a>
            </div>
          )}
          {refreshMsg && <p className="text-xs mt-2 italic text-[#5D7A3A]">{refreshMsg}</p>}
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={downloadCertificate}
          className="w-full text-center bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition mb-3"
        >
          📄 Télécharger le certificat d'antériorité (PDF)
        </button>

        {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
          <a
            href={downloadUrl()}
            className="block w-full text-center bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition mb-6"
          >
            📂 Découvrir l'œuvre originale
          </a>
        )}

        <div className="border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-6 mt-6 text-center">
          <Link
            href="/seal"
            className="inline-block bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition"
          >
            Sceller mon œuvre gratuitement
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/oracle/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

type Message = {
  role: 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
  sources?: any[];
  captures?: number;
};

export default function OraclePage() {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState('');
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const pubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  async function ask() {
    if (!url.trim() || !question.trim()) return;
    if (!pubkey) { setError('Connecte-toi pour utiliser Oracle.'); return; }

    setError('');
    const userMsg: Message = { role: 'user', content: question };
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, userMsg]);
    setQuestion('');
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/oracle/ask', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, url: url.trim(), question }),
      });

      const d = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        setError(d.error || 'Erreur');
        setMessages(prev => [...prev, { role: 'assistant', content: `❌ ${d.error}` }]);
      } else {
        setMessages(prev => [...prev, {
          role: 'assistant',
          content: d.answer,
          sources: d.sources,
          captures: d.captures_used,
        }]);
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError('Erreur réseau');
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto font-sans w-full min-h-screen flex flex-col">
      
      <div className="mb-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU Oracle</h1>
          <span className="text-[0.6rem] bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest px-2 py-1 rounded">
            Beta
          </span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          Posez une question sur le contenu observé d'un site public.
          Oracle ne dit pas ce qui est vrai — il dit ce qu'il a vu, quand, et avec quelle preuve.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4 shadow-sm">
        <label className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] block mb-2">
          URL à interroger
        </label>
        <input 
          type="url"
          placeholder="https://exemple.com"
          className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-transparent outline-none focus:border-[#4A90C2] text-sm"
          style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
          value={url}
          onChange={e => setUrl(e.target.value)}
          disabled={loading}
        />
        <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2 italic">
          🔒 Uniquement les sites publics en HTTPS. Respect de robots.txt.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4 shadow-sm overflow-y-auto min-h-[400px] max-h-[60vh]">
        {messages.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <div className="text-4xl mb-4">🔮</div>
            <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Posez votre première question</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] max-w-sm mx-auto mb-6">
              Exemples : "Quel est le prix actuel ?" · "Qu'est-ce qui a changé depuis 3 mois ?"
            </p>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-2 max-w-md mx-auto">
              {[
                'Quel est le prix affiché ?',
                'Quels sont les horaires ?',
                'Quelle est la date de la dernière mise à jour ?',
                'Quels sont les produits disponibles ?',
              ].map((sugg, i) => (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  onClick={() => setQuestion(sugg)}
                  className="text-left text-xs p-3 rounded-lg border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:border-[#E76F51] transition"
                  style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
                >
                  {sugg}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {messages.map((m, i) => (
              <div key={i} className={`flex ${m.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
                <div className={`max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl p-4 text-sm ${
                  m.role === 'user' 
                    ? 'bg-[#1A1513] text-white rounded-br-sm' 
                    : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-sm'
                }`}>
                  <p className={m.role === 'user' ? '' : 'leading-relaxed'} style={m.role === 'assistant' ? {color: 'var(--text-main)'} : {}}>
                    {m.content}
                  </p>
                  {m.sources && m.sources.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-[var(--border-color)]">
                      <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">
                        Sources ({m.captures} capture{m.captures && m.captures > 1 ? 's' : ''})
                      </p>
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        {m.sources.map((s: any, j: number) => (
                          <div key={j} className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                            • {new Date(s.date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')} 
                            {s.is_live && <span className="ml-1 text-[#3F8F5C]">(live)</span>}
                            <span className="text-[0.55rem] block">Hash : {s.hash_short}...</span>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {loading && (
              <div className="flex justify-start">
                <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm p-4">
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-[var(--text-muted)] rounded-full animate-bounce"></span>
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-[var(--text-muted)] rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{animationDelay: '0.1s'}}></span>
                    <span className="w-2 h-2 bg-[var(--text-muted)] rounded-full animate-bounce" style={{animationDelay: '0.2s'}}></span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-3 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder={url ? 'Posez votre question...' : "Renseignez d'abord une URL ci-dessus"}
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-[#E76F51] text-sm"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={question}
            onChange={e => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && !loading && ask()}
            disabled={loading || !url.trim()}
          />
          <button
            onClick={ask}
            disabled={loading || !url.trim() || !question.trim()}
            className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-[#C75A22] transition shrink-0"
          >
            {loading ? '...' : '→'}
          </button>
        </div>
        {error && <p className="text-xs text-red-500 font-bold mt-2">{error}</p>}
        <p className="text-[0.6rem] text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2 italic">
          💡 Oracle sépare le fait (contenu observé + empreinte) de la lecture (résumé IA).
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 text-center">
        <Link href="/archive" className="text-xs font-bold text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline">
          ← Surveiller un site avec SCEAU Archive
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/profil/AccessLogModal.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function AccessLogModal({ sealId, onClose }: { sealId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState('recent');

  useEffect(() => {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/seal/${sealId}/access-log?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey || '')}&search=${search}&sort=${sort}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setEntries(d.entries || []); setLoading(false); });
  }, [sealId, search, sort]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>📖 Journal des accès</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-xl opacity-50">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Chercher un pseudo..." 
            className="flex-1 border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-main)]"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={search}
            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
          <select 
            className="border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold outline-none bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={sort}
            onChange={e => setSort(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="recent">Vues récentes</option>
            <option value="newest_account">Comptes les + récents</option>
            <option value="oldest_account">Comptes les + anciens</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {loading ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Chargement...</p> :
           entries.length === 0 ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-8">Aucun accès enregistré pour le moment.</p> :
           <div className="space-y-2">
             {entries.map((e, i) => (
               <div key={i} className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                 <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                   <span className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{e.viewer}</span>
                   <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                     {e.viewer_profile.account_age_days < 7 && <span className="text-[0.55rem] bg-red-100 text-red-600 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded uppercase">Nouveau</span>}
                     <span className={`text-[0.6rem] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${e.action === 'download' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'bg-[#DCE8F5] text-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>{e.action}</span>
                   </div>
                 </div>
                 <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                   {new Date(e.at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')} · compte de {e.viewer_profile.account_age_days} jours · {e.viewer_profile.seals_count} sceaux
                 </div>
               </div>
             ))}
           </div>
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
