'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Reset() {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // On vide toute la mémoire du téléphone
    localStorage.clear();
    // On attend 2 secondes et on renvoie vers l'accueil
    setTimeout(() => router.push('/'), 2000);
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-5xl mb-4 animate-spin">⏳</div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Nettoyage en cours...</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Fermeture de la session précédente. Redirection...</p>
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/seal/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { buildMerkleRoot } from '@/lib/merkle';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function SealContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isPactMode = searchParams.get('pact') === 'true';
  const chatId = searchParams.get('chat_id');
  const chatType = searchParams.get('chat_type');
  const challengeIdParam = searchParams.get('challenge_id');

  const [isFlashMode, setIsFlashMode] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media' | 'audio' | 'project'>(
    (searchParams.get('mode') === 'project' ? 'project' : 'file') as any
  );
  const [projectFiles, setProjectFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [pactFiles, setPactFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);

  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState('public');
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isMystery, setIsMystery] = useState(false);
  
  const [unlockLikes, setUnlockLikes] = useState(10);
  const [revealAt, setRevealAt] = useState('');
  const [requiredSignatures, setRequiredSignatures] = useState(2);
  const [pactTheme, setPactTheme] = useState('serieux');

  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sealedResult, setSealedResult] = useState<any>(null);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        setFile(new File([audioBlob], `audio-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));
        setAudioUrl(URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob));
      };
      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch { setStatus(" Impossible d'accéder au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSeal() {
    setStatus('');

    if (!title.trim()) return setStatus(" Un titre est requis");
    if (mode === 'project' && projectFiles.length === 0) return setStatus(" Sélectionne des fichiers pour ton projet");
    if (isPactMode && pactFiles.length === 0) return setStatus(" Ajoute au moins un fichier au pacte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'text' && !textContent.trim()) return setStatus(" Écris ton texte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus(" Choisis un fichier à sceller");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus(" Prends une photo ou une vidéo");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && !file) return setStatus(" Enregistre un audio");

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Préparation du pacte..." : "Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    try {
      let hashContent: Uint8Array;
      let projectManifest: any[] = [];
      let merkleRoot = '';

      if (mode === 'project') {
        // On calcule les hashs de tous les fichiers localement
        for (const f of projectFiles) {
          const buf = await f.arrayBuffer();
          const h = sha256(new Uint8Array(buf));
          const hB64 = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...h));
          projectManifest.push({ name: f.name, hash: hB64, size: f.size });
        }
        merkleRoot = buildMerkleRoot(projectManifest.map(m => m.hash));
        // Le hash principal du sceau (pour l'affichage) sera le Merkle Root
        hashContent = new Uint8Array(Array.from(atob(merkleRoot), c => c.charCodeAt(0)));
      } else if (isPactMode) {
        const metaString = `${title}-${desc}-${pactFiles.map(f => f.name).join(',')}`;
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(metaString));
      } else if (mode === 'text') {
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textContent));
      } else {
        if (!file) return setStatus(" Fichier manquant");
        hashContent = sha256(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()));
      }

      let hashStr = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < hashContent.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hashContent[i]);
      if (mode === 'project') {
        await sendToServer(merkleRoot, merkleRoot, projectManifest);
      } else {
        await sendToServer(btoa(hashStr));
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus(" Erreur de gravure : " + (e?.message || 'inconnue'));
    }
  }

  async function sendToServer(hashB64: string, merkleRoot?: string, projectManifest?: any[]) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return setStatus(" Connecte-toi d'abord !");

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', hashB64);
    formData.append('title', title);
    formData.append('description', mode === 'text' ? textContent : desc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);

    let endpoint = '/api/seal';

    if (mode === 'project') {
      endpoint = '/api/projects';
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      formData.append('merkle_root', merkleRoot as string); // On ajoute le root calculé client
      formData.append('manifest', JSON.stringify(projectManifest || []));
      projectFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else if (isPactMode) {
      endpoint = '/api/pacts/create';
      formData.append('group_id', chatType === 'group' ? (chatId as string) : '');
      formData.append('required_signatures', requiredSignatures.toString());
      formData.append('theme', pactTheme);
      pactFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else {
      formData.append('isFlash', isFlashMode.toString());
      formData.append('visibility', visibility);
      formData.append('isMystery', isMystery.toString());
      formData.append('isEphemeral', 'false');
      formData.append('unlockLikes', unlockLikes.toString());
      formData.append('reveal_at', revealAt);
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      if (challengeIdParam) formData.append('challenge_id', challengeIdParam);
      if (mode !== 'text' && file) formData.append('file', file);
    }

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Envoi du pacte..." : "Ancrage dans la blockchain (max 8s)…");

    try {
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as any));

      if (!res.ok) {
        return setStatus(" " + (data?.error || `Erreur serveur (${res.status})`));
      }

      if (isPactMode) {
        const msg = `[PACTE]${data.id}|${data.title}`;
        if (chatType === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), receiver_handle: chatId, content: msg }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: chatId, sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), content: msg }) });
        }
        window.location.href = '/messages';
      } else {
        setStatus(" Scellé !");
        setSealedResult({ title, hash: hashB64, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'), isFlash: isFlashMode, ots: !!data.ots_secured });
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus(" Erreur réseau : " + (e?.message || 'impossible de contacter le serveur'));
    }
  }

  if (sealedResult) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 font-sans text-center flex flex-col items-center max-w-md mx-auto">
        <div className="text-6xl mb-4">{sealedResult.isFlash ? '⚡' : ''}</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>C'est gravé.</h1>
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] w-full text-left shadow-sm mt-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{sealedResult.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono break-all text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)]">{sealedResult.hash}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-right mt-3 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Scellé le {sealedResult.date}</p>
          {sealedResult.ots && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold"> Preuve horodatée</p>}
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="mt-8 bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full">Sceller une autre œuvre</button>
        <Link href="/feed" className="mt-3 border border-[var(--border-soft)] text-[var(--text-main)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full text-center">Voir le Feed</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 font-sans max-w-md md:max-w-4xl mx-auto w-full">

      {/* === ONGLETS SCeller / Vérifier === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
            <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">Sceller</Link>
            <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Vérifier</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
          {isPactMode ? "Nouveau Pacte." : (isFlashMode ? "Flash." : "L'Atelier.")}
        </h1>
      </div>

      {/* === Toggle Normal / Flash === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(false); setMode('file'); setIsHosted(true); setVisibility('public'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${!isFlashMode ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Standard</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(true); setIsHosted(false); setVisibility('private'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Mode Flash</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">

        {/* === Sélecteur de mode === */}
        {!isPactMode && (
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Fichier</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Texte</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Image</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'audio' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Audio</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('project')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'project' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: mode === 'project' ? '' : 'var(--text-main)' }}>Projet</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Titre === */}
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Titre</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1"
            style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            value={title}
            onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={isPactMode ? "Nom du pacte" : (isFlashMode ? "Ton idée en un mot" : "Titre de l'œuvre")}
          />
        </div>

        {/* === Pacte : fichiers === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Médias (multi-fichiers)</label>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setPactFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
            {pactFiles.length > 0 && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">{pactFiles.length} fichier(s) sélectionné(s)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Description (sauf text) === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode !== 'text' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Description</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1" rows={2} value={desc} onChange={e => setDesc(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Texte === */}
        {mode === 'text' && !isPactMode && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Texte à sceller</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-4 outline-none bg-transparent min-h-[100px] font-mono text-sm mt-1" value={textContent} onChange={e => setTextContent(e.target.value)} placeholder="Écris ton texte ici..." />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Fichier === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Fichier</label>
            <input type="file" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Fichier : {file.name} ({(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} Ko)</p>}
          </div>
        )}
        
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'project' && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: '#A88620' }}>Dossier (Multi-fichiers)</label>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#A88620] mb-2 italic">SCEAU va créer un Arbre de Merkle et ancrer une seule preuve pour tout le dossier.</p>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setProjectFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
            {projectFiles.length > 0 && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold">Fichier : {projectFiles.length} fichier(s) sélectionné(s)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Photo / vidéo === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Photo / Vidéo</label>
            <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Fichier : {file.name}</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Audio === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Audio</label>
            <div className="mt-1">
              {!isRecording && !audioUrl && <button type="button" onClick={startRecording} className="w-full bg-red-500 text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs">Démarrer l'enregistrement</button>}
              {isRecording && <button type="button" onClick={stopRecording} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs animate-pulse">Arrêter</button>}
              {audioUrl && !isRecording && <audio controls src={audioUrl} className="w-full mt-2 h-10" />}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Options (non-flash, non-pacte) === */}
        {!isFlashMode && !isPactMode && mode !== 'project' && (
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-3 mt-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Visibilité</label>
              <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Public</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Amis</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Privé</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isHosted} onChange={e => setIsHosted(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Héberger sur le Cloud</span>
            </label>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isMystery} onChange={e => setIsMystery(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Sceau Mystère</span>
            </label>

            {isMystery && (
              <div className="pl-7">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] block">Likes requis pour révéler</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value={unlockLikes} onChange={e => setUnlockLikes(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
              </div>
            )}

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Éphémère (24h)</span>
            </label>

            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Capsule Temporelle</label>
              <input 
                type="date" 
                value={revealAt} 
                onChange={e => setRevealAt(e.target.value)} 
                className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-[var(--bg-surface)] text-sm focus:border-[#E76F51] outline-none transition cursor-pointer [color-scheme:dark]" 
                style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} 
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Pacte : signatures === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>L'émotion du Pacte</label>
              <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setPactTheme('serieux')} className={`p-2 rounded-xl border-2 text-2xl transition ${pactTheme === 'serieux' ? 'border-[#E76F51] bg-[#FDF4F0]' : 'border-[var(--border-color)] opacity-50'}`} title="Sérieux">🤝</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setPactTheme('amour')} className={`p-2 rounded-xl border-2 text-2xl transition ${pactTheme === 'amour' ? 'border-pink-500 bg-pink-50' : 'border-[var(--border-color)] opacity-50'}`} title="Amour">❤️</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setPactTheme('famille')} className={`p-2 rounded-xl border-2 text-2xl transition ${pactTheme === 'famille' ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50' : 'border-[var(--border-color)] opacity-50'}`} title="Famille">🏡</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setPactTheme('amitie')} className={`p-2 rounded-xl border-2 text-2xl transition ${pactTheme === 'amitie' ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-50' : 'border-[var(--border-color)] opacity-50'}`} title="Amitié">🍻</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Signatures requises</label>
            <input type="number" min="2" value={requiredSignatures} onChange={e => setRequiredSignatures(parseInt(e.target.value) || 2)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-2 rounded-lg text-center font-bold text-lg bg-transparent mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
          </div>
        </div>
        )}

        {/* === Bouton SCELLER === */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSeal}
          className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-white shadow-sm mt-2 ${isPactMode ? 'bg-[#4A90C2]' : (isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810]' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]')}`}
        >
          {isPactMode ? 'CRÉER LE PACTE' : (isFlashMode ? 'GRAVER EN 1 CLIC' : 'SCELLER MON ŒUVRE')}
        </button>

        {/* ===  Affichage du statut — CE QUI MANQUAIT === */}
        {status && (
          <p className={`text-center font-bold text-sm p-3 rounded-lg border ${status.includes('') ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border-[#A8C77E]' : status.includes('') ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] border-[#FCE0D6]' : 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border-[#C9D8E8]'}`}>
            {status}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Seal() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}><SealContent /></Suspense>;
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/settings/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Settings() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');

  const [oldHandle, setOldHandle] = useState('');
  const [newHandle, setNewHandle] = useState('');
  const [bio, setBio] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('createur');
  const [openToCollab, setOpenToCollab] = useState(false);
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState('');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState('');
  const [birthDate, setBirthDate] = useState('');
  const [identityCount, setIdentityCount] = useState(0);
  const [identityHistory, setIdentityHistory] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pubkey, setPubkey] = useState('');
  const [apiKeys, setApiKeys] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newKeyName, setNewKeyName] = useState('');
  const [generatedKey, setGeneratedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isGeneratingKey, setIsGeneratingKey] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!h || !p) { router.push('/login'); return; }

    setOldHandle(h);
    setNewHandle(h);
    setPubkey(p);

    fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.handle) {
          setBio(d.bio || '');
          setRole(d.role || 'createur');
          setOpenToCollab(d.open_to_collaboration === true);
          setFirstName(d.first_name || '');
          setLastName(d.last_name || '');
          setBirthDate(d.birth_date ? d.birth_date.split('T')[0] : '');
          setIdentityCount(d.identity_changes_count || 0);
          setIdentityHistory(d.identity_history || []);
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [router]);

  async function saveProfile() {
    setSaving(true); setMsg('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/profil/update', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        pubkey,
        old_handle: oldHandle,
        new_handle: newHandle,
        bio,
        role,
        open_to_collaboration: openToCollab,
        first_name: firstName,
        last_name: lastName,
        birth_date: birthDate,
      }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();

    if (res.ok) {
      setMsg('✅ Profil mis à jour !');
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.new_handle);
      setOldHandle(data.new_handle);
    } else {
      setMsg('❌ ' + data.error);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function loadApiKeys() {
    if (!pubkey) return;
    const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/api-keys?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
    if (res.ok) {
      const d = await res.json();
      setApiKeys(d.keys || []);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pubkey) loadApiKeys();
  }, [pubkey]);

  async function generateApiKey() {
    if (!newKeyName.trim()) return alert("Donne un nom à ta clé (ex: WordPress, GitHub)");
    setIsGeneratingKey(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/api-keys', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, name: newKeyName })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setGeneratedKey(data.key);
      setNewKeyName('');
      loadApiKeys();
    } else {
      alert('Erreur: ' + data.error);
    }
    setIsGeneratingKey(false);
  }

  async function revokeApiKey(id: string) {
    if (!confirm('Voulez-vous vraiment révoquer cette clé ? Les plugins qui l\'utilisent cesseront de fonctionner.')) return;
    const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/api-keys?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}&id=${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    if (res.ok) loadApiKeys();
    else alert('Erreur lors de la révocation');
  }

  function logout() {
    localStorage.clear();
    router.push('/login');
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-2xl md:max-w-4xl mx-auto font-sans w-full">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <Link href="/profil" className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">←</Link>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Paramètres.</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>👤 Identité publique</h2>

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
          <input type="text" value={newHandle} onChange={e => setNewHandle(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, ''))} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Bio / Description</label>
          <textarea value={bio} onChange={e => setBio(e.target.value)} rows={3} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} placeholder="Parle de toi en quelques mots..." />

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Ton rôle</label>
          <select value={role} onChange={e => setRole(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-soft)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-transparent mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
            <option value="createur">Créateur / Artiste</option>
            <option value="freelance">Freelance</option>
            <option value="entreprise">Entreprise / Agence</option>
            <option value="etudiant">Étudiant</option>
          </select>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer bg-[#F0F6FC] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#C9D8E8]">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={openToCollab} onChange={e => setOpenToCollab(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#4A90C2]" />
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E]">🤝 Ouvert aux collaborations / partenariats</span>
          </label>

          <button onClick={saveProfile} disabled={saving} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold disabled:opacity-50">
            {saving ? 'Sauvegarde...' : 'Sauvegarder les modifications'}
          </button>
          {msg && <p className="text-center font-bold mt-3" style={{color: msg.includes('✅') ? '#7A9B5A' : '#C44A2E'}}>{msg}</p>}
        </div>

        <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border ${identityCount >= 3 ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200' : 'bg-[#F0F6FC] border-[#C9D8E8]'}`}>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
            <h2 className={`text-lg font-bold ${identityCount >= 3 ? 'text-red-700' : 'text-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>🛡️ Identité de confiance (Privée)</h2>
            {identityCount > 0 && (
              <div className="relative group cursor-help">
                <span className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs border border-orange-200">
                  ⚠️ {3 - identityCount} modification(s) restante(s)
                </span>
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-full mt-2 w-64 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs p-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl hidden group-hover:block z-50">
                  <p className="font-bold mb-1 border-b border-gray-700 pb-1">Historique des changements</p>
                  {identityHistory.map((h: any, i: number) => (
                    <p key={i} className="mt-1 opacity-80">Le {new Date(h.date).toLocaleDateString()} : {h.old_name} → {h.new_name}</p>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className={`text-xs mb-4 italic ${identityCount >= 3 ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>
            Ces informations servent uniquement à prouver que ce compte vous appartient. <strong>Vous ne pouvez les modifier que 3 fois maximum.</strong>
          </p>
          
          <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Prénom</label>
              <input type="text" disabled={identityCount >= 3} value={firstName} onChange={e => setFirstName(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:bg-gray-100" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Nom</label>
              <input type="text" disabled={identityCount >= 3} value={lastName} onChange={e => setLastName(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:bg-gray-100" />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Date de naissance</label>
          <input type="date" disabled={identityCount >= 3} value={birthDate} onChange={e => setBirthDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:bg-gray-100" />
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2" style={{color: '#A88620'}}>🔑 Clé cryptographique</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-3">C'est l'empreinte de ton identité souveraine sur SCEAU. Garde-la secrète.</p>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA] overflow-x-auto">
            <code className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">{pubkey}</code>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#F0F6FC] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#C9D8E8]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2 text-[#2D6A9E]">🔌 Clés API (Développeurs & Plugins)</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E] mb-4 italic">Générez une clé pour connecter SCEAU à votre site WordPress, GitHub ou une application externe.</p>

          {generatedKey && (
            <div className="bg-[#FFFCF7] border border-[#E9C46A] p-4 rounded-xl mb-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#A88620] mb-1">⚠️ Copiez cette clé maintenant, elle ne sera plus jamais affichée :</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <code className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded text-xs break-all border border-[var(--border-color)]">{generatedKey}</code>
                <button onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(generatedKey); alert('Copié !'); }} className="bg-[#E9C46A] text-white px-3 rounded font-bold text-xs">Copier</button>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setGeneratedKey(null)} className="text-xs text-[#A88620] font-bold mt-2 underline">J'ai copié la clé, la fermer.</button>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 mb-4">
            <input 
              type="text" 
              placeholder="Nom (ex: Mon site WordPress)" 
              className="flex-1 min-w-0 border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-2 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none text-sm" 
              value={newKeyName} 
              onChange={e => setNewKeyName(e.target.value)} 
            />
            <button 
              onClick={generateApiKey} 
              disabled={isGeneratingKey}
              className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 shrink-0"
            >
              {isGeneratingKey ? '...' : 'Générer'}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#C9D8E8]">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-3">Plugins pour Professionnels</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <a href="/downloads/sceau-wordpress.zip" download className="flex items-center justify-center bg-[#2D6A9E] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow hover:bg-[#1E507A] transition">
                Télécharger pour WordPress
              </a>
              <a href="/downloads/sceau-github-action.yml" download className="flex items-center justify-center bg-[#1A1513] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow hover:bg-black transition">
                Télécharger pour GitHub
              </a>
            </div>
          </div>

          {apiKeys.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2 mt-4 border-t border-[#C9D8E8] pt-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-2">Vos clés actives :</p>
              {apiKeys.map(k => (
                <div key={k.id} className="flex justify-between items-center bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#C9D8E8] text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold">{k.name}</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="text-[#8A7A72]">
                      {k.last_used_at ? `Utilisée le ${new Date(k.last_used_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}` : 'Jamais utilisée'}
                    </span>
                    <button onClick={() => revokeApiKey(k.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 font-bold" title="Révoquer">✕</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#C9D8E8]">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-3">📦 Télécharger les Plugins (SCEAU Pro)</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <a href="/downloads/sceau-wordpress.zip" download className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#2D6A9E] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow hover:bg-[#1E507A] transition">
                <span>🔌</span> Plugin WordPress
              </a>
              <a href="/downloads/sceau-github-action.yml" download className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-[#2C1810] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow hover:bg-black transition">
                <span>🐙</span> GitHub Action (YML)
              </a>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[#2D6A9E] mt-2 italic text-center">Installez le plugin et collez-y votre clé API.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#FDF4F0] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#D64545]">

FICHIER : ./src/app/signup/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Signup() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle || !email || !password) return setStatus("Tous les champs sont requis");
    setStatus("Création en cours...");
    
    try {
      const secret = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const pubkey = ed25519.getPublicKey(secret);
      let pubkeyStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < pubkey.length; i++) pubkeyStr += String.fromCharCode(pubkey[i]);
      let secretStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < secret.length; i++) secretStr += String.fromCharCode(secret[i]);
      const pubkeyB64 = btoa(pubkeyStr);

      localStorage.setItem('sceau_secret', btoa(secretStr));

      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/signup', { 
        method: 'POST', 
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
        body: JSON.stringify({ handle, email, password, pubkey: pubkeyB64 }) 
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok) {
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', pubkeyB64);
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', handle);
        setStatus("✅ Compte créé !");
        setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
      } else {
        setStatus("❌ " + (data.error || "Une erreur est survenue"));
      }
    } catch (e) { setStatus("❌ Impossible de contacter le serveur. Vérifiez votre connexion."); }
  }

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSignup(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un compte</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
      <input type="text" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "🙈" : "👁️"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleSignup} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">S'inscrire</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Déjà un compte ? <Link href="/login" className="underline font-bold">Se connecter</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/u/[handle]/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function PublicProfile() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h);
    if (params.handle) {
      fetch(`/api/user-profile/${params.handle}?viewer=${h || ''}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => { setData(d); setLoading(false); });
    }
  }, [params.handle]);

  async function addFriend() {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target: data.handle }) });
    alert(`Demande d'ami envoyée à @${data.handle} !`);
    // Recharger pour mettre à jour le bouton
    window.location.reload();
  }

  // Couleurs par catégorie pour rendre la grille vivante
  const getCategoryStyle = (category: string) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'Art': return 'border-[#E9C46A] bg-[#FDF8F0]';
      case 'Musique': return 'border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]';
      case 'Code': return 'border-[#2A9D8F] bg-[#F0F9F7]';
      case 'Écrit': return 'border-[#A78BC8] bg-[#F5F0FA]';
      default: return 'border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]';
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement du profil...</div>;
  if (data?.error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{data.error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20 font-serif">
      
      {/* EN-TÊTE DU PROFIL PUBLIC */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] pt-12 pb-8 px-6 text-center relative">
        <div className="w-28 h-28 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-4xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-xl">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{data.handle}</h1>
        {data.open_to_collaboration && (
          <span className="inline-block mt-2 bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">
            🤝 Ouvert aux collaborations
          </span>
        )}
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">{data.role || 'Créateur sur SCEAU'}</p>
        
        {myHandle !== data.handle && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            {data.relationship === 'friend' && <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">✅ Vous êtes amis</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'pending' && <span className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">⏳ Demande en attente</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'none' && <button onClick={addFriend} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-8 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">+ Ajouter à mon cercle</button>}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 text-sm font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          <span>{data.seals.length} Œuvres</span>
          <span>{data.seals.filter((s:any)=>s.is_hosted).length} Fichiers</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LA VITRINE (GRILLE) */}
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 mt-8">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          🖼️ Galerie Publique
        </h2>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${seal.id}`} key={seal.id} className={`block p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 transition duration-200 ${getCategoryStyle(seal.category)}`}>
              
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold italic opacity-60" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  {seal.category}
                </span>
                {seal.is_hosted ? <span title="Fichier hébergé">📁</span> : <span title="Preuve seule">🔒</span>}
              </div>

              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2 leading-tight" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</h3>
              
              {seal.description && (
                <p className="text-xs italic opacity-70 line-clamp-2 mb-3" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  "{seal.description}"
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono opacity-50 border-t border-black/5 pt-2 mt-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          
          {data.seals.length === 0 && (
            <div className="col-span-full text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Ce créateur n'a pas encore publié d'œuvre publique.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/verify/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link'; // L'IMPORT MANQUANT EST ICI
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export default function Verify() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('file');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [titleQuery, setTitleQuery] = useState('');
  
  const [timelineData, setTimelineData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [timelineLoading, setTimelineLoading] = useState(true);
  const [timelineMode, setTimelineMode] = useState<'live' | 'period'>('live');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab !== 'timeline') return;
    const fetchTimeline = () => {
      setTimelineLoading(true);
      const url = timelineMode === 'live' 
        ? '/api/verify/timeline?live=true'
        : `/api/verify/timeline?start=${startDate}&end=${endDate}`;
        
      fetch(url).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
        setTimelineData(data.timeline || []);
        setTimelineLoading(false);
      });
    };
    fetchTimeline();
    if (timelineMode === 'live') {
      const interval = setInterval(fetchTimeline, 5000);
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [tab, timelineMode, startDate, endDate]);

  async function verifyFile(file: File) {
    setLoading(true);
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: btoa(hashStr) }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function verifyHash(input: string) {
    if(!input) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: input }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function searchByTitle() {
    if(!titleQuery) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ query: titleQuery }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  const timeAgo = (dateStr: string) => {
    const diff = Math.floor((new Date().getTime() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()) / 60000);
    if (diff < 1) return "À l'instant";
    if (diff === 1) return "Il y a 1 minute";
    if (diff < 60) return `Il y a ${diff} minutes`;
    return new Date(dateStr).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR');
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 pb-32 md:pb-6 max-w-2xl md:max-w-5xl mx-auto font-sans w-full">
      <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">✍️ Sceller</Link>
          <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">🔍 Vérifier</Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2 text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Vérifier & Découvrir.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-6 text-center">Prouvez l'authenticité ou découvrez le monde.</p>
      
      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-6">
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('file');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='file'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Fichier</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('hash');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='hash'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Hash</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('title');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='title'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Titre</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('timeline');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='timeline'?'bg-[#E76F51] text-white shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: tab==='timeline' ? '#FFF' : 'var(--text-main)'}}>Chronologie</button>
      </div>

      {tab === 'file' && (
        <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-xl text-center">
          <input type="file" onChange={e=>e.target.files?.[0] && verifyFile(e.target.files[0])} className="w-full text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'hash' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Collez le hash (ex: b2e7...)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none font-mono bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onChange={e=>verifyHash(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'title' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Titre de l'œuvre (ex: Mon premier roman)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={titleQuery} onChange={e=>setTitleQuery(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && searchByTitle()} />
          <button onClick={searchByTitle} className="w-full bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Rechercher</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'timeline' && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          
          {/* Filtres de la frise */}
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
            <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-lg">
              <button onClick={() => setTimelineMode('live')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md transition ${timelineMode === 'live' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>En direct</button>
              <button onClick={() => setTimelineMode('period')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md transition ${timelineMode === 'period' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Par période</button>
            </div>
            
            {timelineMode === 'period' && (
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => setStartDate(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
                <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => setEndDate(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {timelineLoading ? (
            <p className="text-center font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Synchronisation...</p>
          ) : timelineData.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucun sceau sur cette période.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="relative pl-6">
              {/* La ligne verticale */}
              <div className="absolute left-2.5 top-2 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-[#E76F51] opacity-30"></div>

              <div className="space-y-6">
                {timelineData.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="relative animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Le point */}
                    <div className="absolute -left-[1.35rem] top-1.5 w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] border-2 border-[var(--bg-main)] shadow-sm"></div>
                    
                    <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-1">
                        <span className="font-bold text-xs" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{item.author}</span>
                        <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{timeAgo(item.timestamp)}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{item.title}</div>
                      <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">{item.category}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab !== 'pulse' && result && (
        <div className={`mt-8 p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 ${result.found ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'bg-[#FDF4F0] border-[#D64545]'}`}>
          {result.found ? (
            <div>
              <div className="text-center mb-6">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2"></div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-xl" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{tab === 'title' ? 'ŒUVRES TROUVÉES' : 'FICHIER AUTHENTIQUE'}</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">{result.seals.length} preuve(s)</p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {result.seals.map((seal: any, index: number) => (
                  <div key={index} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${index === 0 ? 'border-[#E76F51] bg-[var(--bg-main)]' : 'border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                          Par <span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{seal.author}</span> le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
                        </div>
                        {seal.type === 'project' && (
                          <div className="mt-2 inline-block bg-[#F0F6FC] border border-[#C9D8E8] text-[#2D6A9E] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold">
                            🛡️ Fait partie du dossier : {seal.project_title}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        {seal.hash && <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2 break-all">Hash: {seal.hash}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
                      <a href={seal.file_path} target="_blank" className="mt-3 inline-block bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">⬇️ Télécharger l'œuvre</a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-center font-bold text-[#A02E2E]"> Aucune œuvre trouvée.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/components/Cockpit.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Cockpit() {
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<any>(null);
  const [activities, setActivities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pendingPacts, setPendingPacts] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setHandle(h);

    if (!pubkey) { setLoading(false); return; }

    // Profil
    fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { if (d.handle) setProfile(d); })
      .catch(() => {});

    // Activité (rafraîchie toutes les 30s)
    const fetchActivity = () => {
      fetch(`/api/profil/activity?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setActivities(d.activities || []))
        .catch(() => {});
    };
    fetchActivity();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchActivity, 30000);

    // Pactes en attente
    fetch(`/api/pacts/pending?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => setPendingPacts(d.count || 0))
      .catch(() => {});

    setLoading(false);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // Stockage dynamique
  const maxQuotaGB = 0.1; // 100 Mo Free
  const usedBytes = profile?.used_bytes || 0;
  const usedGB = (usedBytes / (1024 * 1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2);
  const percentage = Math.min(100, (parseFloat(usedGB) / maxQuotaGB) * 100);

  // Équipe dynamique (toi + amis)
  const teamCount = 1 + (profile?.friends_count || 0);
  const teamMax = 1; // Free = 1 siège, PRO = 5

  function timeAgo(dateStr: string) {
    const diff = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()) / 60000);
    if (diff < 1) return "à l'instant";
    if (diff < 60) return `il y a ${diff} min`;
    if (diff < 1440) return `il y a ${Math.floor(diff/60)}h`;
    return `il y a ${Math.floor(diff/1440)}j`;
  }

  return (
    <aside className="hidden lg:flex flex-col w-[340px] bg-[var(--bg-main)] border-l border-[var(--border-color)] h-full overflow-y-auto shrink-0">
      
      {/* BLOC 1 : STATUT & QUOTAS RÉELS */}
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Mon Espace Pro</h3>
        
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-4 shadow-sm mb-4">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-2">
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-main)]">Stockage</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{usedGB} Go / {maxQuotaGB} Go</span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full bg-[var(--border-color)] rounded-full h-2 mb-4 overflow-hidden">
            <div className={`h-2 rounded-full ${percentage > 90 ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-[#4A90C2]'}`} style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}></div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-3">
            <div>
              <p className="font-bold text-[var(--text-main)]">{profile?.sealed || 0}</p>
              <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)]">Sceaux au total</p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="font-bold text-[var(--text-main)]">Équipe</p>
              <Link href="/friends" className="text-[var(--orange)] hover:underline">{teamCount} / {teamMax} siège{teamCount > 1 ? 's' : ''}</Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {profile && (
          <Link href="/profil" className="w-full bg-white border border-[var(--border-color)] text-[var(--text-main)] py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#E0EDF8] transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            Voir mon profil complet
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* BLOC 2 : JOURNAL D'ACCÈS (Traqueur DYNAMIQUE) */}
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex-1">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-muted)]">Traqueur d'accès</h3>
          <span className="flex h-2 w-2 relative">
            <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-green-400 opacity-75"></span>
            <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-green-500"></span>
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {!handle ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Connectez-vous pour voir l'activité.</p>
          ) : loading ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Chargement...</p>
          ) : activities.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucun accès récent. Partagez vos œuvres pour voir l'activité ici.</p>
          ) : (
            activities.map((a, i) => (
              <Link 
                key={i} 
                href={`/o/${a.seal_id}`}
                className={`block p-3 rounded-lg border transition hover:border-[var(--orange)] ${a.is_new_account ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] border-[#FCE0D6]' : 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <span className="text-lg">{a.action === 'download' ? '⬇️' : '👁️'}</span>
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${a.is_new_account ? 'text-[#C44A2E]' : 'text-[var(--text-main)]'}`}>
                      @{a.viewer} {a.action === 'download' ? 'a téléchargé' : 'a vu'}{' '}
                      <span className="italic">"{a.seal_title?.slice(0, 25)}..."</span>
                    </p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                      <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[var(--text-muted)]">{timeAgo(a.created_at)}</p>
                      {a.is_new_account && (
                        <span className="text-[0.55rem] bg-red-100 text-red-600 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">Nouveau compte</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
        {handle && activities.length > 0 && (
          <Link href="/profil" className="block w-full mt-4 text-xs font-bold text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline text-center">
            Voir tout le journal des accès →
          </Link>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* BLOC 3 : PLAN DE TRAVAIL DYNAMIQUE */}
      <div className="p-6 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)]">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-3">À faire</h3>
        <div className="space-y-2 mb-6">
          <Link href="/messages" className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition group">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-lg"></span>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-main)]">
                {pendingPacts > 0 ? `${pendingPacts} Pacte${pendingPacts > 1 ? 's' : ''} en attente` : 'Aucun pacte en attente'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[var(--text-muted)] group-hover:text-[var(--orange)]">→</span>
          </Link>

          <Link href="/settings" className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition group">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-lg"></span>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-main)]">Connecter un plugin</span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-[var(--text-muted)] group-hover:text-[var(--orange)]">→</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#1A1513] p-4 rounded-lg text-center shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="relative z-10">
            <p className="text-[#EADFD4] font-bold text-sm mb-1">SCEAU PRO</p>
            <p className="text-[0.65rem] text-[#8A7A72] mb-3">Débloquez l'illimité.</p>
            <Link href="/pricing" className="inline-block bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[var(--orange-hover)] transition">
              Mettre à niveau
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/components/Manifeste.tsx

import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Manifeste() {
  return (
    <div className="mt-16 mb-8 max-w-xl mx-auto px-4">

        {/* Élément décoratif */}

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <span className="text-3xl mb-2 block">⚖️</span>

            La justice a besoin d'yeux.

            </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3 items-start">
            <div className="text-[#E76F51] mt-1 font-bold">2.</div>

              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Tu l'as eu avant. L'horodatage cryptographique est infalsifiable et vérifiable par tous.</p>
            </div>

          <div className="flex gap-3 items-start">
            <div className="text-[#E76F51] mt-1 font-bold">3.</div>
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Le journal des accès</h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">L'autre l'a vu. Les traces de téléchargement et de consultation sont conservées.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] text-center">
          <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
            "Ce n'est pas la loi du plus fort. C'est la loi de la preuve."
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/db/notifications.sql
=========================================

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notifications (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  actor_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type TEXT NOT NULL,
  message TEXT NOT NULL,
  link TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_read BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/db/pool.ts
=========================================

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { requireDb } from '@/lib/env';

let _pool: Pool | null = null;

function create(): Pool {
  const url = requireDb();
  const pool = new Pool({
    connectionString: url,
    max: 5,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 10_000,
    ssl: { rejectUnauthorized: true },
  });
  pool.on('error', (err) => {
    console.error('[pg] idle client error:', err.message);
  });
  return pool;
}

export function getPool(): Pool {
  if (!_pool) _pool = create();
  return _pool;
}

// Compat : `import { pool } from '@/db/pool'` continue de fonctionner.
export const pool = new Proxy({} as Pool, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    const real = getPool() as any;
    const v = real[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(real) : v;
  },
});

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  email TEXT UNIQUE,
  password_hash TEXT,
  pubkey BYTEA NOT NULL,
  recovery_email TEXT,
  reset_token TEXT,
  reset_expiry TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  hash BYTEA NOT NULL,
  timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  title TEXT,
  description TEXT,
  category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art',
  is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
  is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  file_path TEXT,
  file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT unique_seal UNIQUE (hash, author_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  sender_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  receiver_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS likes (
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY (seal_id, user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pacts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  creator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  file_path TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'en_attente',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pact_signatures (
  pact_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pacts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (pact_id, user_id)
);

FICHIER : ./src/lib/api-error.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

function isDbError(e: any): boolean {
  const code = e?.code;
  if (['ECONNREFUSED', 'ENOTFOUND', 'ETIMEDOUT', '28P01', '3D000', '57P01'].includes(code)) return true;
  return /ECONNREFUSED|ENOTFOUND|timeout|connect.*pool|database/i.test(String(e?.message || ''));
}

export function apiError(e: unknown, fallback = 'Erreur serveur'): NextResponse {
  if (isDbError(e)) {
    console.error('[api-error] base injoignable :', (e as any)?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Base de données injoignable. Réessayez dans un instant.' },
      { status: 503 }
    );
  }
  console.error('[api-error]', e);
  return NextResponse.json({ error: fallback }, { status: 500 });
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/env.ts

// Validation centralisée des variables d'environnement.
// Échoue TÔT avec un message clair au lieu de produire `undefined/undefined/clé`.

function clean(v: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!v) return undefined;
  return v.trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '');
}

function required(name: string): string {
  const v = clean(process.env[name]);
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Variable d'environnement manquante ou vide : ${name}`);
  return v;
}

function optional(name: string, def = ''): string {
  return clean(process.env[name]) ?? def;
}

export function requireS3(): { endpoint: string; bucket: string; region: string } {
  const endpoint = required('AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3').replace(/\/$/, '');
  const bucket = required('S3_BUCKET').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
  const region = optional('AWS_REGION', 'us-west-004');
  return { endpoint, bucket, region };
}

export function requireDb(): string {
  return required('DATABASE_URL');
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/lib/merkle.ts
=========================================

import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

/**
 * Prend une liste de hashs (en Base64) et calcule le Hash Racine (Merkle Root).
 * Utilise Uint8Array pour éviter les conflits de types avec Buffer.
 */
export function buildMerkleRoot(hashesB64: string[]): string {
  if (hashesB64.length === 0) return '';
  if (hashesB64.length === 1) return hashesB64[0];

  // Convertir les hashs Base64 en Uint8Array
  let currentLevel: Uint8Array[] = hashesB64.map(h => {
    const binaryString = atob(h);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    return bytes;
  });

  while (currentLevel.length > 1) {
    const nextLevel: Uint8Array[] = [];
    
    for (let i = 0; i < currentLevel.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < currentLevel.length) {
        // Concaténer les deux hashs
        const combined = new Uint8Array(currentLevel[i].length + currentLevel[i + 1].length);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i], 0);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i + 1], currentLevel[i].length);
        
        // Calculer le hash du résultat
        const hash = sha256(combined);
        nextLevel.push(hash);
      } else {
        // S'il reste un élément impair, on le passe au niveau supérieur
        nextLevel.push(currentLevel[i]);
      }
    }
    currentLevel = nextLevel;
  }

  // Convertir le Uint8Array final en Base64
  const root = currentLevel[0];
  let binary = '';
  const len = root.byteLength;
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(root[i]);
  }
  
  return btoa(binary);
}
