FICHIER : ./src/lib/ots.ts
=========================================

// En-tête .ots complet (31 octets) :
// \x00 + "OpenTimestamps" (14) + \x00\x00 + "Proof" (5) + \x00 + 8 octets magiques
const HEADER_MAGIC = Buffer.from(
  '004f70656e54696d657374616d7073000050726f6f6600bf89e2e884e89294',
  'hex'
);

const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

const CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000;
const MAX_PROOF_BYTES = 64 * 1024;

export type OtsResult = {
  ok: boolean;
  proof?: Buffer;
  calendars: string[];
  error?: string;
};

function toDetachedOts(hash: Uint8Array, calendarBody: Buffer): Buffer {
  if (calendarBody.length >= HEADER_MAGIC.length &&
      calendarBody.subarray(0, HEADER_MAGIC.length).equals(HEADER_MAGIC)) {
    return calendarBody;
  }
  // Si le calendar a renvoyé une preuve partielle sans header, on l'encapsule proprement
  const hashBuffer = Buffer.from(hash);
  return Buffer.concat([
    HEADER_MAGIC,
    Buffer.from([0x01, 0x08]), // version 1 + op SHA-256
    hashBuffer,
    calendarBody,
  ]);
}

async function submitOne(url: string, hash: Uint8Array): Promise<Buffer> {
  const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream',
      Accept: 'application/vnd.opentimestamps.v1',
      'User-Agent': 'sceau/1.0',
    },
    body: Buffer.from(hash),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS),
    cache: 'no-store',
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
  const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  if (buf.length === 0 || buf.length > MAX_PROOF_BYTES) throw new Error('taille invalide');
  if (buf[0] === 0x3c || buf[0] === 0x7b) throw new Error('réponse non binaire (HTML/JSON)');
  return buf;
}

export async function submitToOts(hash: Uint8Array): Promise<OtsResult> {
  if (hash.length !== 32) return { ok: false, calendars: [], error: 'hash_invalid_length' };

  const settled = await Promise.allSettled(CALENDARS.map((u) => submitOne(u, hash)));

  const successes: string[] = [];
  let body: Buffer | undefined;
  settled.forEach((r, i) => {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled') {
      successes.push(CALENDARS[i]);
      if (!body) body = r.value;
    }
  });

  if (!body) return { ok: false, calendars: successes, error: 'all_calendars_failed' };
  return { ok: true, proof: toDetachedOts(hash, body), calendars: successes };
}

// Upgrade : interroge les calendars avec le hash de l'ENGAGEMENT contenu dans la preuve
// (pas le hash du fichier — sinon ça ne confirme jamais).
export async function upgradeOts(hash: Uint8Array, proofBase64: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  let engagement: Uint8Array = hash;
  try {
    const raw = Buffer.from(proofBase64, 'base64');
    if (raw.length > HEADER_MAGIC.length + 34 &&
        raw.subarray(0, HEADER_MAGIC.length).equals(HEADER_MAGIC)) {
      const version = raw[HEADER_MAGIC.length];
      const op = raw[HEADER_MAGIC.length + 1];
      if (version === 0x01 && op === 0x08) {
        engagement = raw.subarray(HEADER_MAGIC.length + 2, HEADER_MAGIC.length + 34);
      }
    }
  } catch { /* fallback hash */ }

  const hashHex = Buffer.from(engagement).toString('hex');
  const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
    CALENDARS.map(async (url) => {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/timestamp/${hashHex}`, {
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS),
        cache: 'no-store',
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      if (buf.length === 0 || buf.length > MAX_PROOF_BYTES) throw new Error('taille invalide');
      return buf;
    })
  );

  let best: Buffer | null = null;
  for (const r of settled) {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled' && (!best || r.value.length > best.length)) best = r.value;
  }
  return best;
}

import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf';

const APP_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'https://sceau-app.vercel.app';

export function generateCertificate(
  title: string,
  author: string,
  date: string,
  hashBase64: string,
  qrCodeDataUrl: string,
  hashHex?: string,
  sealId?: string
) {
  // Convertir en hex si non fourni
  // Sécurisation côté client (pas de Buffer.from)
  const hash = hashHex || (() => {
    try {
      const raw = atob(hashBase64);
      let hex = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
        hex += raw.charCodeAt(i).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
      }
      return hex;
    } catch {
      return hashBase64;
    }
  })();

  const hashChunks = hash.toUpperCase().match(/.{1,32}/g) || [hash];

  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'landscape', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  // Fond et cadre
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 297, 210, 'F');
  doc.setDrawColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 277, 190);

  // Titre
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(32);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT D'ANTÉRIORITÉ", 148.5, 38, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("Délivré par le protocole SCEAU", 148.5, 48, { align: 'center' });

  // Métadonnées
  doc.setFontSize(13);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`Titre : ${title}`, 25, 68);
  doc.text(`Créateur : @${author}`, 25, 76);
  doc.text(`Date de scellement : ${new Date(date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}`, 25, 84);

  // Hash hex complet
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("EMPREINTE SHA-256 (hexadécimal) :", 25, 96);

  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(90, 75, 68);
  hashChunks.forEach((chunk: string, i: number) => {
    doc.text(chunk, 25, 104 + i * 5);
  });

  // Mention OTS
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
  doc.text("Ancrage temporel : OpenTimestamps (blockchain Bitcoin)", 25, 145);

  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("La preuve .ots est téléchargeable depuis la page publique de l'œuvre", 25, 152);
  doc.text("et vérifiable indépendamment sur https://opentimestamps.org", 25, 158);

  // QR code
  if (qrCodeDataUrl) {
    doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 215, 60, 60, 60);
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    doc.text("Scannez pour vérifier en ligne", 245, 125, { align: 'center' });
  }

  // Lien de vérification
  if (sealId) {
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
    const url = `${APP_URL}/o/${sealId}`;
    doc.textWithLink(`Vérifier : ${url}`, 25, 175, { url });
  }

  // Mention légale
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'italic');
  doc.setFontSize(7);
  doc.setTextColor(150, 150, 150);
  doc.text(
    "Document généré par SCEAU. Le hash SHA-256 est infalsifiable : toute modification, même d'un seul bit, change le hash.",
    25,
    188
  );

  doc.save(`SCEAU-${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

export function generatePortfolioPDF(
  author: string,
  seals: any[],
  startDate?: string,
  endDate?: string,
  isPro: boolean = false
) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  doc.setFillColor(245, 233, 216);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(36);
  doc.setTextColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.text('PORTFOLIO DE PREUVE', 105, 110, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(22);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`@${author}`, 105, 128, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(11);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  let dateText = `Généré le ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  if (startDate && endDate) {
    dateText += ` | Période : du ${new Date(startDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} au ${new Date(endDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  }
  doc.text(dateText, 105, 142, { align: 'center' });
  doc.text(`${seals.length} œuvre(s) certifiée(s)`, 105, 150, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.text(`Vérification : ${APP_URL}/o/[id]`, 105, 162, { align: 'center' });

  doc.addPage();
  let y = 20;

  seals.forEach((seal) => {
    if (y > 230) {
      doc.addPage();
      y = 20;
    }

    doc.setDrawColor(239, 224, 202);
    doc.setLineWidth(0.5);
    doc.rect(15, y, 180, 65);

    doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
    doc.setFontSize(13);
    doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
    doc.text(seal.title || 'Sans titre', 20, y + 10);

    doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
    doc.setFontSize(9);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    const dateStr = new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
    doc.text(`Scellé le : ${dateStr}`, 20, y + 18);

    if (seal.description) {
      doc.setFont('helvetica', 'italic');
      const splitDesc = doc.splitTextToSize(`"${seal.description}"`, 170);
      doc.text(splitDesc.slice(0, 2), 20, y + 26);
    }

    if (seal.has_ots_proof || seal.ots_proof) {
      doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
      doc.setFontSize(8);
      doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
      doc.text('✓ Ancré via OpenTimestamps (Bitcoin)', 20, y + 42);
    }

    doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.textWithLink('[ Vérifier en ligne ]', 20, y + 52, { url: `${APP_URL}/o/${seal.id}` });

    if (seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path) {
      doc.textWithLink('[ Ouvrir le fichier original ]', 110, y + 52, { url: seal.file_path });
    }

    y += 75;
  });

  if (!isPro) {
    const pageCount = doc.getNumberOfPages();
    for (let i = 1; i <= pageCount; i++) {
      doc.setPage(i);
      doc.setFontSize(50);
      doc.setTextColor(220, 200, 180);
      doc.text('SCEAU FREE', 40, 150, { angle: 45 });
    }
  }

  doc.save(`Portfolio-${author}.pdf`);
}

export function generatePactCertificate(
  title: string,
  description: string,
  creator: string,
  signataires: string[],
  hash: string,
  qrCodeDataUrl: string,
  pactId: string,
  theme: string = 'serieux'
) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  // Couleurs selon le thème
  const colors = {
    serieux: { border: [233, 196, 106], bg: [253, 250, 245], title: [44, 24, 16], accent: [168, 134, 32] },
    amour: { border: [244, 114, 182], bg: [253, 242, 248], title: [131, 24, 67], accent: [219, 39, 119] },
    famille: { border: [134, 239, 172], bg: [240, 253, 244], title: [20, 83, 45], accent: [22, 163, 74] },
    amitie: { border: [147, 197, 253], bg: [239, 246, 255], title: [30, 58, 138], accent: [37, 99, 235] },
  };
  const c = colors[theme as keyof typeof colors] || colors.serieux;

  // Fond Parchemin
  doc.setFillColor(c.bg[0], c.bg[1], c.bg[2]);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');

  // Bordure Dorée Élégante
  doc.setDrawColor(c.border[0], c.border[1], c.border[2]);
  doc.setLineWidth(1);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 190, 277);
  doc.rect(12, 12, 186, 273);

  // Titre
  doc.setFont('times', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(32);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT DE PACTE", 105, 35, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFont('times', 'italic');
  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(c.accent[0], c.accent[1], c.accent[2]);
  doc.text("SCEAU · La poignée de main numérique", 105, 45, { align: 'center' });

  // Ligne de séparation
  doc.setDrawColor(c.border[0], c.border[1], c.border[2]);
  doc.line(60, 50, 150, 50);

  // Titre du Pacte
  doc.setFont('times', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(22);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(title, 105, 70, { align: 'center' });

  // Description
  doc.setFont('times', 'italic');
  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(90, 75, 68);
  const splitDesc = doc.splitTextToSize(`"${description}"`, 140);
  doc.text(splitDesc, 105, 85, { align: 'center' });

  // Créateur
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text(`Proposé par : @${creator}`, 105, 110, { align: 'center' });

  // Signatures
  doc.setFont('times', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(16);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("Signataires Officiels", 105, 130, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(63, 143, 92); // Vert
  const sigsText = signataires.map(s => `@${s}`).join('   •   ');
  doc.text(sigsText, 105, 142, { align: 'center' });

  // Le Sceau Visuel (Un cercle rouge en bas à droite)
  doc.setDrawColor(232, 120, 60);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.circle(170, 220, 20);
  doc.setFont('times', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(232, 120, 60);
  doc.text("SCELLÉ", 170, 218, { align: 'center' });
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.text("BITCOIN", 170, 224, { align: 'center' });

  // Hash
  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(7);
  doc.setTextColor(150, 150, 150);
  const hashChunks = hash.match(/.{1,50}/g) || [hash];
  hashChunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
    doc.text(chunk, 105, 250 + (i * 5), { align: 'center' });
  });

  // QR Code
  if (qrCodeDataUrl) {
    doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 25, 200, 40, 40);
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    doc.text("Scanner pour vérifier", 45, 245, { align: 'center' });
  }

  // Mention Blockchain
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
  doc.text("Preuve ancrée via OpenTimestamps (Bitcoin)", 105, 275, { align: 'center' });

  doc.save(`Pacte-${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}


export function generateProjectCertificate(title: string, author: string, merkleRoot: string, files: any[], date: string) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });
  
  // Fond
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');
  
  // Cadre
  doc.setDrawColor(232, 120, 60);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 190, 277);

  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(24);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT D'INTÉGRITÉ", 105, 30, { align: 'center' });
  
  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("Dossier Miroir (Arbre de Merkle)", 105, 40, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`Projet : ${title}`, 20, 55);
  doc.text(`Créateur : @${author}`, 20, 63);
  doc.text(`Date de scellement : ${new Date(date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}`, 20, 71);

  doc.setFont('courier', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.text("EMPREINTE RACINE (MERKLE ROOT) :", 20, 85);
  
  const chunks = merkleRoot.match(/.{1,50}/g) || [merkleRoot];
  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  chunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
    doc.text(chunk, 20, 91 + i * 5);
  });

  // Liste des fichiers
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.text("FICHIERS INCLUS :", 20, 115);
  
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  let y = 122;
  files.slice(0, 30).forEach((f) => {
    if (y > 270) return; // Limite de page
    doc.text(`- ${f.file_name}`, 20, y);
    doc.text(`  Hash: ${f.file_hash.slice(0, 20)}...`, 25, y + 4);
    y += 10;
  });

  if (files.length > 30) {
    doc.text(`... et ${files.length - 30} autres fichiers.`, 20, y + 5);
  }

  doc.save(`Projet-${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

import { S3Client } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { requireS3 } from '@/lib/env';

let _client: S3Client | null = null;

function create(): S3Client {
  const { endpoint, region } = requireS3();
  const accessKeyId = (process.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID || '').trim();
  const secretAccessKey = (process.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY || '').trim();
  if (!accessKeyId || !secretAccessKey) {
    throw new Error('Clés S3 manquantes : AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID / AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY');
  }
  return new S3Client({
    region,
    endpoint,
    credentials: { accessKeyId, secretAccessKey },
    forcePathStyle: true,
    requestChecksumCalculation: 'WHEN_REQUIRED',
    responseChecksumValidation: 'WHEN_REQUIRED',
  });
}

export function getR2Client(): S3Client {
  if (!_client) _client = create();
  return _client;
}

export const r2Client = new Proxy({} as S3Client, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    const real = getR2Client() as any;
    const v = real[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(real) : v;
  },
});


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/lib/robots.ts
=========================================

// Cache mémoire des robots.txt (TTL 1h)
const cache = new Map<string, { rules: string[]; allowAll: boolean; expires: number }>();
const TTL = 3600_000;

export async function isAllowedByRobots(url: string, userAgent = 'SCEAU-Bot'): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    const root = `${u.protocol}//${u.hostname}`;
    const cached = cache.get(root);
    if (cached && cached.expires > Date.now()) {
      return evaluate(cached, u.pathname, userAgent);
    }

    const res = await fetch(`${root}/robots.txt`, {
      headers: { 'User-Agent': userAgent },
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      // Pas de robots.txt = tout autorisé
      const entry = { rules: [], allowAll: true, expires: Date.now() + TTL };
      cache.set(root, entry);
      return true;
    }

    const text = await res.text();
    const lines = text.split('\n').map(l => l.trim());
    const rules: string[] = [];
    let inOurSection = false;
    let allowAll = false;

    for (const line of lines) {
      if (line.startsWith('#') || !line) continue;
      const [key, ...rest] = line.split(':');
      const k = key.trim().toLowerCase();
      const v = rest.join(':').trim();
      
      if (k === 'user-agent') {
        inOurSection = v === '*' || v.toLowerCase().includes('sceau');
        if (inOurSection && v === '*') allowAll = true;
      } else if (inOurSection && (k === 'disallow' || k === 'allow')) {
        rules.push(`${k}:${v}`);
      }
    }

    const entry = { rules, allowAll, expires: Date.now() + TTL };
    cache.set(root, entry);
    return evaluate(entry, u.pathname, userAgent);
  } catch {
    // Erreur réseau : on refuse par prudence
    return false;
  }
}

function evaluate(entry: { rules: string[]; allowAll: boolean }, path: string, _ua: string): boolean {
  // Règles les plus longues d'abord (spécifiques)
  const sorted = [...entry.rules].sort((a, b) => {
    const al = a.split(':').slice(1).join(':').length;
    const bl = b.split(':').slice(1).join(':').length;
    return bl - al;
  });

  for (const rule of sorted) {
    const [type, pattern] = rule.split(':');
    if (!pattern) {
      if (type === 'disallow') return false;
      continue;
    }
    const regex = new RegExp('^' + pattern.replace(/[.+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*'));
    if (regex.test(path)) {
      return type === 'allow';
    }
  }

  return true; // Par défaut, autorisé
}


=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/lib/safe-fetcher.ts
=========================================

// Module unique de téléchargement pour Archive, Cron et Oracle.
// Règles : HTTPS only, pas d'IP internes, robots.txt, timeout, taille max, redirections validées.

import { isAllowedByRobots } from './robots';

const USER_AGENT = 'SCEAU-Bot/1.0 (+https://sceau-app.vercel.app)';
const TIMEOUT_MS = 8000;
const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_REDIRECTS = 3;

function isPrivateIp(hostname: string): boolean {
  const h = hostname.toLowerCase();
  if (h === 'localhost' || h === '127.0.0.1' || h === '::1' || h === '0.0.0.0') return true;
  if (/^10\./.test(h)) return true;
  if (/^192\.168\./.test(h)) return true;
  if (/^172\.(1[6-9]|2[0-9]|3[0-1])\./.test(h)) return true;
  if (/^169\.254\./.test(h)) return true;
  if (/^fc00:/i.test(h) || /^fe80:/i.test(h)) return true;
  return false;
}

function isSafeProtocol(url: URL): boolean {
  return url.protocol === 'https:';
}

export type FetchResult =
  | { ok: true; html: string; finalUrl: string; statusCode: number }
  | { ok: false; error: string; statusCode?: number };

export async function safeFetch(inputUrl: string): Promise<FetchResult> {
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(inputUrl);
  } catch {
    return { ok: false, error: 'URL invalide' };
  }

  if (!isSafeProtocol(url)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'HTTPS obligatoire' };
  }

  if (isPrivateIp(url.hostname)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Adresse interne interdite' };
  }

  if (url.username || url.password) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'URL avec identifiants interdite' };
  }

  // Vérifie robots.txt
  const allowed = await isAllowedByRobots(url.toString(), USER_AGENT);
  if (!allowed) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Bloqué par robots.txt' };
  }

  let currentUrl = url.toString();
  let redirects = 0;

  while (redirects <= MAX_REDIRECTS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(currentUrl, {
        headers: { 'User-Agent': USER_AGENT, 'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml' },
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(TIMEOUT_MS),
        redirect: 'manual',
      });

      // Gère les redirections manuellement
      if (res.status >= 300 && res.status < 400) {
        const location = res.headers.get('location');
        if (!location) return { ok: false, error: 'Redirection sans location', statusCode: res.status };
        
        const nextUrl = new URL(location, currentUrl);
        if (!isSafeProtocol(nextUrl) || isPrivateIp(nextUrl.hostname)) {
          return { ok: false, error: 'Redirection vers destination interdite', statusCode: res.status };
        }
        currentUrl = nextUrl.toString();
        redirects++;
        continue;
      }

      if (!res.ok) {
        return { ok: false, error: `HTTP ${res.status}`, statusCode: res.status };
      }

      // Vérifie Content-Length
      const contentLength = res.headers.get('content-length');
      if (contentLength && parseInt(contentLength) > MAX_BYTES) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'Contenu trop lourd', statusCode: res.status };
      }

      // Lit en streaming avec limite
      const reader = res.body?.getReader();
      if (!reader) return { ok: false, error: 'Pas de body', statusCode: res.status };

      let bytes = 0;
      const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read();
        if (done) break;
        bytes += value.byteLength;
        if (bytes > MAX_BYTES) {
          reader.cancel();
          return { ok: false, error: 'Contenu trop lourd', statusCode: res.status };
        }
        chunks.push(value);
      }

      const html = new TextDecoder().decode(Buffer.concat(chunks));
      return { ok: true, html, finalUrl: currentUrl, statusCode: res.status };
    } catch (e: any) {
      if (e?.name === 'TimeoutError' || e?.name === 'AbortError') {
        return { ok: false, error: 'Timeout' };
      }
      return { ok: false, error: e?.message || 'Erreur réseau' };
    }
  }

  return { ok: false, error: 'Trop de redirections' };
}

export function cleanHtmlToText(html: string): string {
  return html
    .replace(/<script\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<style\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/style>/gim, '')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '\n')
    .replace(/\n\s*\n/g, '\n')
    .trim();
}

export function extractTitle(html: string, fallbackUrl: string): string {
  const t = html.match(/<title[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/title>/i);
  if (t?.[1]) {
    const clean = t[1].replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (clean.length > 0 && clean.length < 200) return clean;
  }
  const h1 = html.match(/<h1[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/h1>/i);
  if (h1?.[1]) {
    const clean = h1[1].replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    if (clean.length > 0 && clean.length < 100) return clean;
  }
  try {
    const u = new URL(fallbackUrl);
    return u.pathname === '/' ? u.hostname : `${u.hostname}${u.pathname}`;
  } catch {
    return fallbackUrl;
  }
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/safe-key.ts
=========================================

export function safeKeyPart(input: string, maxLen = 80): string {
  if (!input) return 'file';
  const ascii = input.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '');
  const cleaned = ascii.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+/g, '-');
  const safe = cleaned.replace(/^\.+/, '').replace(/\.{2,}/g, '.').replace(/-+/g, '-');
  return (safe || 'file').slice(0, maxLen);
}

export function buildS3Key(opts: {
  authorId: string; hashBase64: string; originalName: string; prefix?: string;
}): string {
  const prefix = opts.prefix || 'seal';
  const authorShort = opts.authorId.replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 8);
  const hashShort = opts.hashBase64.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 16);
  const safeName = safeKeyPart(opts.originalName, 80);
  return `${prefix}/${authorShort}/${hashShort}-${safeName}`;
}

export function extractS3Key(filePath: string): string | null {
  const endpoint = (process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3 || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
  const bucket = (process.env.S3_BUCKET || '').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
  const prefix = `${endpoint}/${bucket}/`;
  if (filePath.startsWith(prefix)) return filePath.slice(prefix.length);
  return filePath.split('/').pop() || null;
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/sceau-protocol.ts
=========================================

import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

/**
 * Calcule l'empreinte SHA-256 d'un fichier (Buffer ou Uint8Array).
 */
export function hashDocument(content: Uint8Array): string {
  const hash = sha256(content);
  return Buffer.from(hash).toString('hex');
}

/**
 * Ancre un hash sur la blockchain Bitcoin via OpenTimestamps.
 * Retourne la preuve .ots en base64, prête à être stockée.
 */
export async function anchorToBitcoin(hashHex: string): Promise<{ otsProof: string | null, calendars: string[] }> {
  const hashBytes = Buffer.from(hashHex, 'hex');
  const successes: string[] = [];
  let proof: Buffer | undefined;

  for (const url of CALENDARS) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' },
        body: hashBytes,
      });
      if (!res.ok) continue;

      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      const magic = Buffer.from([0x00,0x4f,0x70,0x65,0x6e,0x54,0x69,0x6d,0x65,0x73,0x74,0x61,0x6d,0x70,0x73,0x00,0x00,0x50,0x72,0x6f,0x6f,0x66,0x00]);
      
      if (!buf.subarray(0, magic.length).equals(magic)) continue;

      successes.push(url);
      if (!proof) proof = buf;
    } catch (e) {
      // Calendar indisponible, on tente le suivant
    }
  }

  return {
    otsProof: proof ? proof.toString('base64') : null,
    calendars: successes
  };
}
